## A1 · Meet and make a plan

Unit reference: A1

### Learning goal

Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs.

Builds on: Start here

### Pattern explanation

Use me llamo for a name, vivo en for residence and me gusta with la música. Soy de gives origin; it does not give the meeting place.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- Hola - hello [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-0.ebc6b606d73f.mp3)
- llamo - am called (in me llamo) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-1.bd28c6663b81.mp3)
- vivo - I live [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-2.bc8512d98153.mp3)
- México - Mexico [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-3.5b92ef42f8e9.mp3)
- casa - house / home [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-4.ed4d103c57b5.mp3)
- pequeña - small (feminine singular) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-5.28455c4356bf.mp3)
- música - music [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-6.e1121663c882.mp3)
- mañana - tomorrow [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-7.e32b2cbea0e6.mp3)

### Additional source language

- cómo — how, in ¿cómo te llamas?
  - ¿Cómo te llamas?
- dónde — where
  - ¿Dónde vives?
- el — the, masculine singular article
  - Nos vemos mañana en el parque.
- hasta — until / see you, in hasta mañana
  - ¡Hasta mañana!
- llamas — are called, addressing tú in te llamas
  - ¿Cómo te llamas?
- ocho — eight
  - A las ocho.
- parque — park
  - Nos vemos mañana en el parque.
- Rosa — a person’s name
  - Me llamo Rosa.
- te — part of te llamas when asking someone’s name
  - ¿Cómo te llamas?
- vives — you live, addressing tú
  - ¿Dónde vives?
- sí — yes, with an accent; si without an accent means if / whether
  - Sí, ¡hasta mañana!

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- ¡Hola! — Hello!
- Me llamo Ana. — My name is Ana.
- Soy de México. — I am from Mexico.
- Vivo en la Ciudad de México. — I live in Mexico City.
- Me gusta la música. — I like music.
- Nos vemos mañana. — See you tomorrow.
- A las siete. — At seven o’clock.
- Mi casa es pequeña. — My home is small.

### Teaching notes

- Use a short sentence for each fact. The reader should be able to find your name, place or plan easily.
- A name or place can change without changing the useful sentence pattern. Start from what you know.
- Read your message aloud, then check whether the person receiving it has the information they need.

### Reading source

¡Hola! Me llamo Ana. Soy de México. Vivo en la Ciudad de México. Mi casa es pequeña. Me gusta la música. Nos vemos mañana. A las siete.

English guide:
Hello! My name is Ana. I am from Mexico. I live in Mexico City. My home is small. I like music. See you tomorrow, at seven.

### Listening source

¡Hola! Me llamo Rosa. Vivo en la Ciudad de México. Me gusta la música. Nos vemos mañana en el parque. A las ocho. ¡Hasta mañana!

English guide:
Hello! My name is Rasa/Rosa. I live in Vilnius/Mexico City. I like music. Let us meet tomorrow in the park, at eight. See you tomorrow!

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.listen.7396ad956be6.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. What is the main purpose?
   - Introduce someone and arrange a meeting **[key]**
   - Complain about a hotel
2. When is the meeting?
   - Tomorrow at seven **[key]**
   - Today at eight
3. Which detail describes the writer’s home?
   - It is small **[key]**
   - It has a garden
   - It is near a station

### Listening task key

1. When is the listening meeting?
   - Tomorrow at eight **[key]**
   - Tomorrow at seven
   - Today at eight
2. Where will they meet?
   - At home
   - In the park **[key]**
   - At the station
3. What interest does the speaker mention?
   - Cooking
   - Football
   - Music **[key]**

### Spoken production

Introduce a real or invented person, say where they live and mention an interest. Then invite your listener to meet and give a time.

- Give a name and a place.
- State an interest.
- Make the day and time clear.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¡Hola! ¿Cómo te llamas?
   - Meaning: Hello! What is your name?
   - Response goal: Introduce yourself.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.turn-0.00030dc01754.mp3)
2. ¿Dónde vives?
   - Meaning: Where do you live?
   - Response goal: Say where you live.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.turn-1.ecbd3be027f2.mp3)
3. ¿Nos vemos mañana a las ocho?
   - Meaning: Shall we meet tomorrow at eight?
   - Response goal: Accept the invitation or propose another time.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.turn-2.b7bc9ed3b47f.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Tell a friend how the reading and listening meeting plans differ. Make both meeting times clear.

### Writing assignments

#### Introduce yourself

Reader: A new member of your language group

Write a short introduction. Use your own details or an invented profile.

Suggested length: 8–35 words.

- Give a name.
- Say where you live or are from.
- Mention one interest.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Me llamo Rosa. Soy de México. Me gusta la música.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Give a name
  - Me llamo Rosa.
  - This identifies the person writing.
- Say where you live or are from
  - Soy de México.
  - This supplies origin; a residence would also meet this goal.
- Mention an interest
  - Me gusta la música.
  - This gives the new group member something to talk about.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### A note about home

Reader: A friend who is visiting

Write a brief note describing your home and inviting your friend to meet.

Suggested length: 6–30 words.

- Give one simple fact about your home.
- Suggest meeting tomorrow.
- Include a meeting time.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Mi casa es pequeña. Nos vemos mañana. A las ocho.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Give a simple fact about home
  - Mi casa es pequeña.
  - This gives the visitor one useful description.
- Suggest meeting tomorrow
  - Nos vemos mañana.
  - This proposes the meeting day.
- Give the meeting time
  - A las ocho.
  - This makes the arrangement usable; a day alone is incomplete.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### Arrange a first meeting

Reader: Someone you have just met

Write a friendly message arranging to meet.

Suggested length: 8–30 words.

- Begin with a greeting.
- Introduce yourself briefly.
- Give a day and a time.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
¡Hola! Me llamo Ana. Nos vemos mañana. A las siete.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Greet and introduce yourself
  - ¡Hola! Me llamo Ana.
  - The reader can identify who is contacting them.
- Give a day
  - Nos vemos mañana.
  - This supplies tomorrow as the meeting day.
- Give a time
  - A las siete.
  - This supplies the time without changing the day.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

### Required practical table reading

A table gives meaning through both its headings and the position of each cell. Find the requested column before following its row.
The row labels distinguish original details from the changed or requested details. A proposed replacement is not proof that a real booking has been confirmed.
The second task turns the table: field labels run down the side. Compare the table with the displayed message before selecting an answer.
- Original details: Nombre = Ana; Ciudad = Ciudad de México; Hora de la reunión = a las siete
- Changed / requested details: Nombre = Rosa; Ciudad = Ciudad de México; Hora de la reunión = a las ocho

#### Read across the rows

Layout: rows

Displayed comparison source:
¡Hola! Me llamo Ana. Soy de México. Vivo en la Ciudad de México. Mi casa es pequeña. Me gusta la música. Nos vemos mañana. A las siete.

| Source details | Nombre | Ciudad | Hora de la reunión |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Original details | Ana | Ciudad de México | a las siete |
| Changed / requested details | Rosa | Ciudad de México | a las ocho |

- In the original details, what belongs under Nombre?
  - Ana **[key]**
  - Rosa
  - Ciudad de México
  - a las siete
  - Explanation: Nombre means name. Follow that column to the original details row.
- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Hora de la reunión?
  - a las ocho **[key]**
  - a las siete
  - Ana
  - Ciudad de México
  - Explanation: Hora de la reunión means meeting time. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- Compare the two rows. Which details change?
  - Name + Meeting time **[key]**
  - Name
  - City
  - Meeting time
  - None of the details
  - Explanation: Changed fields: Name + Meeting time. Unchanged: City.

#### Compare a message with the table

Layout: columns

Displayed comparison source:
¡Hola! Me llamo Rosa. Vivo en la Ciudad de México. Me gusta la música. Nos vemos mañana en el parque. A las ocho. ¡Hasta mañana!

| Field | Original details | Changed / requested details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Nombre | Ana | Rosa |
| Ciudad | Ciudad de México | Ciudad de México |
| Hora de la reunión | a las siete | a las ocho |

- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Nombre?
  - Rosa **[key]**
  - Ana
  - Ciudad de México
  - a las siete
  - Explanation: Nombre means name. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- In the original details, what belongs under Ciudad?
  - Ciudad de México **[key]**
  - Ana
  - a las siete
  - Explanation: Ciudad means city. Follow that column to the original details row.
- Which table column matches the displayed changed / requested message?
  - Original details
  - Changed / requested details **[key]**
  - Explanation: Use the changed / requested details column. The other column records the original source.

### Required practical forms

The labels and field formats are explicitly taught. Both prepared-source forms are required; detail checking is not an open-writing accuracy grade.
Vivo en la Ciudad de México gives the city. In a city field, write Ciudad de México. Keep a las with the meeting time.

Label preparation:
- Nombre — Name; original: Ana; changed: Rosa
- Ciudad — City; original: Ciudad de México; changed: Ciudad de México
- Hora de la reunión — Meeting time; original: a las siete; changed: a las ocho

#### Record the original details

Source: prepared reading

¡Hola! Me llamo Ana. Soy de México. Vivo en la Ciudad de México. Mi casa es pequeña. Me gusta la música. Nos vemos mañana. A las siete.

Field key:
- Nombre: Ana
- Ciudad: Ciudad de México
- Hora de la reunión: a las siete

#### Record the changed source details

Source: prepared message shown as text

¡Hola! Me llamo Rosa. Vivo en la Ciudad de México. Me gusta la música. Nos vemos mañana en el parque. A las ocho. ¡Hasta mañana!

Field key:
- Nombre: Rosa
- Ciudad: Ciudad de México
- Hora de la reunión: a las ocho

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A1:confirm

Question purpose: At eight?

Prepared question: ¿A las ocho?

Partner message: A las ocho.

Detail check: Which time did your partner clarify?

Key: Eight

Optional response frame: A las ocho.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified time so your partner can hear you understood.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1:correct

Question purpose: At seven?

Prepared question: ¿A las siete?

Partner message: Mañana. A las ocho.

Detail check: Which time did your partner clarify?

Key: Eight

Optional response frame: A las ocho.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified time so your partner can hear you understood.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask a short question to check the meeting time, then respond to the clarified time.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: ¿A las ocho?
  - Meaning: At eight?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A1.question-confirm.900b57701086.mp3)

- Checking question: ¿A las siete?
  - Meaning: At seven?

  - Partner reply: Mañana. A las ocho.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A1.correct.0a51f2063938.mp3)

<a id="a1-shopping"></a>
## A1 · Buy everyday things

Unit reference: A1-shopping

### Learning goal

Understand an item, quantity and price, and make a short purchase request.

Builds on: A1

### Pattern explanation

Dos manzanas gives a quantity; treinta pesos gives a price. Quisiera pan is a polite request. Voy a pagar con tarjeta gives the payment method.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- tienda - shop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-0.1e2490a845d8.mp3)
- manzanas - apples [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-1.6ccf0f8e0360.mp3)
- dos - two [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-2.367fa57b38c5.mp3)
- treinta - thirty [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-3.07189528f84e.mp3)
- pan - bread [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-4.ac0addde528a.mp3)
- cuestan - cost (plural) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-5.74e6c5b244d7.mp3)
- tarjeta - card [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-6.ab7cf9340c56.mp3)
- efectivo - cash [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-7.035cd29f145a.mp3)

### Additional source language

- algo — something / anything
  - ¿Desea algo más?
- bien — well / okay, in está bien
  - Está bien.
- buenos — part of buenos días: good morning
  - ¡Buenos días!
- compro — I buy / am buying
  - En la tienda compro dos manzanas y pan.
- cuarenta — forty
  - ¿Cuarenta pesos?
- desea — you would like / want, polite usted
  - ¿Qué desea?
- días — days, in the greeting buenos días
  - ¡Buenos días!
- está — is, in está bien: that is okay
  - Está bien.
- más — more / else
  - ¿Desea algo más?
- o — or
  - ¿Va a pagar con tarjeta o en efectivo?
- qué — what
  - ¿Qué desea?
- tres — three
  - Quisiera tres manzanas.
- va — you are going to, polite usted in va a pagar
  - ¿Va a pagar con tarjeta o en efectivo?
- y — and
  - En la tienda compro dos manzanas y pan.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- tienda — shop
- manzanas — apples
- dos manzanas — two apples
- treinta pesos — three euros / thirty pesos
- Quisiera pan. — I would like bread.
- ¿Cuánto cuesta? — How much does it cost?
- Voy a pagar con tarjeta. — I will pay by card.
- Gracias, ¡hasta luego! — Thank you, goodbye!

### Teaching notes

- Say the item and quantity clearly. A price and an item count answer different questions.
- Use quisiera for a polite request. Keep the noun plural after dos or tres: dos manzanas, tres manzanas.
- The Lithuanian case uses euros; the Mexican Spanish case uses pesos. Keep each source’s amount separate.

### Reading source

En la tienda compro dos manzanas y pan. Las manzanas cuestan treinta pesos. Voy a pagar con tarjeta. Gracias, ¡hasta luego!

English guide:
At the shop I buy two apples and bread. The apples cost three euros / thirty pesos. I will pay by card. Thank you, goodbye!

### Listening source

¡Buenos días! Quisiera tres manzanas. ¿Cuánto cuestan? ¿Cuarenta pesos? Está bien. Voy a pagar en efectivo. ¡Gracias!

English guide:
The customer asks for three apples, confirms a price of four euros / forty pesos, and pays in cash.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.listen.cb9888578476.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. How many apples are bought?
   - Two **[key]**
   - Three
   - Four
2. How will the customer pay?
   - In cash
   - By card **[key]**
   - With a voucher
3. What other item is bought?
   - Milk
   - Tea
   - Bread **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. How many apples are requested?
   - Two
   - Three **[key]**
   - Four
2. How will this customer pay?
   - In cash **[key]**
   - By card
   - The friend will pay
3. Which amount is confirmed?
   - Thirty pesos
   - Ten pesos
   - Forty pesos **[key]**

### Spoken production

Describe a small shopping trip, including the items, one quantity and your payment method.

- Name the items.
- Keep quantities and prices distinct.
- Make the payment method clear.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¡Buenos días! ¿Qué desea?
   - Meaning: Hello! What would you like?
   - Response goal: Request an item and give a quantity.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.turn-0.a223a255f704.mp3)
2. ¿Desea algo más?
   - Meaning: Would you like anything else?
   - Response goal: Add one item or politely say that is all.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.turn-1.d5902ee51130.mp3)
3. ¿Va a pagar con tarjeta o en efectivo?
   - Meaning: Will you pay by card or in cash?
   - Response goal: State your payment method and close politely.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.turn-2.4e9c88d6b280.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Tell a friend the difference between the two customers’ purchases and payment methods.

### Writing assignments

#### A shopping message

Reader: A friend buying things for you

Write a short message asking your friend to buy everyday items. Use real or invented details.

Suggested length: 6–25 words.

- Name two items.
- Give at least one quantity.
- Add a polite closing.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Dos manzanas. Quisiera pan. Gracias, ¡hasta luego!

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Name an item and quantity
  - Dos manzanas.
  - A short shopping note can list an item and its quantity without a full request sentence.
- Name a different item
  - Quisiera pan.
  - The friend needs two different items; repeating apples twice does not add a second item.
- Close politely
  - Gracias, ¡hasta luego!
  - This closes the short note politely.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A short shopping note: item, price and planned payment

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

  - Model detail: Cuarenta pesos.
- How will the shopper pay?
  - Model detail: Con tarjeta.

#### three-thirty-cash

  - Model detail: Tres manzanas.
- What is the total price in pesos?
  - Model detail: Treinta pesos.
- How will the shopper pay?
  - Model detail: En efectivo.

<a id="a1-home"></a>
## A1 · Welcome someone to your home

Unit reference: A1-home

### Learning goal

Understand simple room descriptions and give a visitor useful information.

Builds on: A1-shopping

### Pattern explanation

Mi casa es pequeña describes a quality with agreement. Aquí está la cocina gives a location. La llave está sobre la mesa locates the key.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- casa - home [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-4.ed4d103c57b5.mp3)
- cocina - kitchen [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-1.d0f0f9713789.mp3)
- cuarto - room [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-2.979ceee2b34c.mp3)
- baño - bathroom [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-3.0b5dae7b1f7a.mp3)
- llave - key [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-4.0cd869cd9ca1.mp3)
- mesa - table [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-5.7760fc069262.mp3)
- silla - chair [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-6.b6d6aa07f999.mp3)
- agua - water [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.word-7.9858d4d3c6bc.mp3)

### Additional source language

- bolsa — bag
  - ¿Dónde puedo dejar mi bolsa?
- dar — give
  - ¿Me puedes dar agua?
- dejar — leave / put, in the question about the bag
  - ¿Dónde puedo dejar mi bolsa?
- departamento — apartment, common in Mexico
  - Mi departamento está en el segundo piso.
- dónde — where
  - ¿Dónde está la cocina?
- este — this, masculine singular
  - Este cuarto es grande y la cocina es pequeña.
- grande — big / large
  - Este cuarto es grande y la cocina es pequeña.
- junto — part of junto a: next to
  - El baño está junto a la cocina.
- luminosa — bright, feminine singular with casa
  - Mi casa es pequeña, pero luminosa.
- pero — but
  - Mi casa es pequeña, pero luminosa.
- piso — floor / storey
  - Mi departamento está en el segundo piso.
- puedes — you can, informal tú
  - ¿Me puedes dar agua?
- puedo — I can
  - ¿Dónde puedo dejar mi bolsa?
- segundo — second, with piso
  - Mi departamento está en el segundo piso.
- tu — your, informal singular; no accent in the possessive
  - Tu llave está sobre la silla.
- y — and
  - Este cuarto es grande y la cocina es pequeña.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Mi casa es pequeña. — My home is small.
- Aquí está la cocina. — Here is the kitchen.
- El cuarto es luminoso. — The room is bright.
- El baño está aquí. — The bathroom is here.
- Pasa, por favor. — Please come in.
- Siéntate aquí. — Sit here.
- ¿Quieres un té? — Would you like tea?
- La llave está sobre la mesa. — The key is on the table.

### Teaching notes

- Use a short sentence to locate each room or object.
- Use es for a quality and está for a location: mi casa es pequeña, el baño está aquí. Adjectives agree with the noun.
- For a visitor, useful information is more important than listing every object in the home.

### Reading source

¡Hola! Pasa, por favor. Mi casa es pequeña, pero luminosa. Aquí está la cocina. El baño está junto a la cocina. La llave está sobre la mesa. ¿Quieres un té?

English guide:
The host welcomes a visitor to a small, bright home. The bathroom is beside the kitchen. The key is on the table, and the host offers tea.

### Listening source

¡Hola! Mi departamento está en el segundo piso. Pasa. Este cuarto es grande y la cocina es pequeña. Tu llave está sobre la silla. ¿Quieres agua?

English guide:
The flat is on the second floor. The room is large and the kitchen small. The visitor’s key is on the chair, and the host offers water.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.listen.2ce5f4688ebe.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. Where is the bathroom?
   - Beside the kitchen **[key]**
   - Outside the building
   - Beside the bedroom
2. Where is the key?
   - On the chair
   - On the table **[key]**
   - In a bag
3. What drink is offered?
   - Coffee
   - Water
   - Tea **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. Which floor is mentioned?
   - The first
   - The second **[key]**
   - The third
2. Where is this key?
   - On the chair **[key]**
   - On the table
   - By the door
3. How is the kitchen described?
   - Large
   - Dark
   - Small **[key]**

### Spoken production

Welcome a visitor and describe a real or invented home.

- Describe one room.
- Locate a useful object.
- Make a simple offer to the visitor.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Dónde está la cocina?
   - Meaning: Where is the kitchen?
   - Response goal: Tell your visitor where the kitchen is.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.turn-0.13253065a076.mp3)
2. ¿Dónde puedo dejar mi bolsa?
   - Meaning: Where can I put my bag?
   - Response goal: Suggest a simple place for the bag.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.turn-1.5a04f78049df.mp3)
3. ¿Me puedes dar agua?
   - Meaning: Could I have water?
   - Response goal: Respond politely to the visitor’s request.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-home.turn-2.b65064081b75.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Leave a voice note telling the visitor where to find the key in each of the two homes. Do not mix up the table and chair.

### Writing assignments

#### A note for a visitor

Reader: A friend arriving at your home

Write a useful arrival note for a real or invented home.

Suggested length: 8–35 words.

- Welcome the visitor.
- Describe where to find a room or object.
- Give one useful instruction or offer.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Pasa, por favor. La llave está sobre la silla. ¿Quieres un té?

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Welcome the visitor
  - Pasa, por favor.
  - This tells the visitor they can come in.
- Locate a useful object
  - La llave está sobre la silla.
  - This tells the visitor where to find the key.
- Make a simple offer
  - ¿Quieres un té?
  - This offers a drink; an appropriate useful instruction could also meet this goal.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

<a id="a1-travel"></a>
## A1 · Find a stop and buy a ticket

Unit reference: A1-travel

### Learning goal

Understand simple directions, a departure time and a ticket request.

Builds on: A1-home

### Pattern explanation

Siga and dé vuelta are polite usted commands. A la derecha and a la izquierda give opposite directions. A las diez gives a time, not a destination.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- parada - bus stop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-0.91036fe2da47.mp3)
- autobús - bus [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-1.55c73da44908.mp3)
- derecho - straight ahead [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-2.609b935f9e54.mp3)
- derecha - right (direction) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-3.3be32014e5b3.mp3)
- izquierda - left (direction) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-4.7b751a9072c7.mp3)
- boleto - ticket [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-5.4cd4bab5d8e8.mp3)
- llega - arrives [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-6.d1198a253834.mp3)
- once - eleven [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-7.710d5d1f35a9.mp3)

### Additional source language

- adónde — where to
  - ¿Adónde quiere ir?
- al — a + el: to / by the, in junto al parque
  - La parada está junto al parque.
- boletos — tickets
  - ¿Cuántos boletos quiere?
- comprar — buy
  - Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.
- compre — buy, polite usted instruction
  - Compre el boleto en el autobús.
- cuántos — how many, with masculine plural boletos
  - ¿Cuántos boletos quiere?
- después — then / afterwards
  - Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda.
- ir — go
  - ¿Adónde quiere ir?
- junto — part of junto a: next to
  - La parada está junto al parque.
- parque — park
  - La parada está junto al parque.
- puede — you can, polite usted
  - Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.
- quiere — you want, polite usted
  - ¿Adónde quiere ir?
- sabe — you know, polite usted
  - ¿Sabe dónde está la parada?
- y — and
  - Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- parada — bus stop
- terminal — station / bus terminal
- Siga derecho. — Go straight.
- Dé vuelta a la derecha. — Turn right.
- Dé vuelta a la izquierda. — Turn left.
- ¿Dónde está la parada? — Where is the stop?
- Un boleto, por favor. — One ticket, please.
- El autobús llega a las diez. — The bus arrives at ten.

### Teaching notes

- Separate the walking directions from the bus destination.
- The examples use polite directions: eikite / siga, sukite / dé vuelta. Follow the complete pattern.
- A stop and a terminal are different places. Check the place, direction and time.

### Reading source

La parada está junto al parque. Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús a la terminal llega a las diez. Compre el boleto en el autobús.

English guide:
The stop is beside the park. Go straight and turn right. The bus to the terminal arrives at ten. Buy the ticket on the bus.

### Listening source

La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda. El autobús a la terminal llega a las once. Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.

English guide:
This stop is beside the shop. Go straight, then turn left. The terminal bus arrives at eleven. A ticket can be bought on the bus.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.listen.17b564dd4285.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. What is beside the stop?
   - The park **[key]**
   - The shop
   - The library
2. Which turn is given?
   - Left
   - Right **[key]**
   - No turn
3. When does the bus arrive?
   - Eleven
   - Nine
   - Ten **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. Which turn is given in the recording?
   - Right
   - Left **[key]**
   - No turn
2. Where is this stop?
   - Beside the shop **[key]**
   - Inside the terminal
   - Beside the library
3. What time is mentioned?
   - Ten
   - Twelve
   - Eleven **[key]**

### Spoken production

Explain how a visitor can find a stop and catch a bus.

- Locate the stop.
- Give a direction.
- State a time and ticket detail.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Adónde quiere ir?
   - Meaning: Where would you like to go?
   - Response goal: State a simple destination.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.turn-0.6838c467fc51.mp3)
2. ¿Sabe dónde está la parada?
   - Meaning: Do you know where the stop is?
   - Response goal: Ask for the stop or check a direction.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.turn-1.bdb5152f87b9.mp3)
3. ¿Cuántos boletos quiere?
   - Meaning: How many tickets would you like?
   - Response goal: Request a quantity and close politely.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.turn-2.3df40ddc2e13.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Tell a visitor how the written and spoken routes differ, especially the turning direction and bus time.

### Writing assignments

#### A short travel note

Reader: A visiting friend

Write simple directions to a real or invented stop, with a useful bus detail.

Suggested length: 12–40 words.

- Locate the stop.
- Give a clear direction.
- Include a time or ticket instruction.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho. Dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús llega a las once.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Locate the stop
  - La parada está junto a la tienda.
  - The landmark identifies where the visitor should go.
- Give a direction
  - Siga derecho. Dé vuelta a la derecha.
  - This gives the walking sequence. Right and left must stay distinct.
- Give a useful bus detail
  - El autobús llega a las once.
  - The arrival time adds a practical transport detail; a learned ticket instruction is another valid choice.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

Directions from a local person

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### park-right-eleven

La parada está junto al parque. Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús a la terminal llega a las once. Compre el boleto en el autobús.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Junto al parque.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: A la derecha.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: A las once.

#### shop-left-ten

La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda. El autobús a la terminal llega a las diez. Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Junto a la tienda.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: A la izquierda.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: A las diez.

### Controlled listening transfer

A local person gives directions and a bus time

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### spoken-1

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A1-travel.spoken-1.b727f8e692b4.mp3)

La parada está junto al parque. Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda. El autobús a la terminal llega a las diez. Compre el boleto en el autobús.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Junto al parque.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: A la izquierda.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: A las diez.

#### spoken-2

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A1-travel.spoken-2.f9a5874cca46.mp3)

La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús a la terminal llega a las once. Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Junto a la tienda.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: A la derecha.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: A las once.

### Required practical table reading

A table gives meaning through both its headings and the position of each cell. Find the requested column before following its row.
The row labels distinguish original details from the changed or requested details. A proposed replacement is not proof that a real booking has been confirmed.
The second task turns the table: field labels run down the side. Compare the table with the displayed message before selecting an answer.
- Original details: Ubicación de la parada = junto al parque; Vuelta = a la derecha; Hora de llegada = a las diez
- Changed / requested details: Ubicación de la parada = junto a la tienda; Vuelta = a la izquierda; Hora de llegada = a las once

#### Read across the rows

Layout: rows

Displayed comparison source:
La parada está junto al parque. Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús a la terminal llega a las diez. Compre el boleto en el autobús.

| Source details | Ubicación de la parada | Vuelta | Hora de llegada |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Original details | junto al parque | a la derecha | a las diez |
| Changed / requested details | junto a la tienda | a la izquierda | a las once |

- In the original details, what belongs under Ubicación de la parada?
  - junto al parque **[key]**
  - junto a la tienda
  - a la derecha
  - a las diez
  - Explanation: Ubicación de la parada means stop location. Follow that column to the original details row.
- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Hora de llegada?
  - a las once **[key]**
  - a las diez
  - junto al parque
  - a la derecha
  - Explanation: Hora de llegada means arrival time. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- Compare the two rows. Which details change?
  - Stop location + Turn + Arrival time **[key]**
  - Stop location
  - Turn
  - Arrival time
  - None of the details
  - Explanation: Changed fields: Stop location + Turn + Arrival time. All three requested details differ.

#### Compare a message with the table

Layout: columns

Displayed comparison source:
La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda. El autobús a la terminal llega a las once. Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.

| Field | Original details | Changed / requested details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ubicación de la parada | junto al parque | junto a la tienda |
| Vuelta | a la derecha | a la izquierda |
| Hora de llegada | a las diez | a las once |

- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Ubicación de la parada?
  - junto a la tienda **[key]**
  - junto al parque
  - a la derecha
  - a las diez
  - Explanation: Ubicación de la parada means stop location. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- In the original details, what belongs under Vuelta?
  - a la derecha **[key]**
  - a la izquierda
  - junto al parque
  - a las diez
  - Explanation: Vuelta means turn. Follow that column to the original details row.
- Which table column matches the displayed changed / requested message?
  - Original details
  - Changed / requested details **[key]**
  - Explanation: Use the changed / requested details column. The other column records the original source.

### Required practical forms

The labels and field formats are explicitly taught. Both prepared-source forms are required; detail checking is not an open-writing accuracy grade.
Keep the complete location chunks junto al parque / junto a la tienda. A la derecha and a la izquierda are opposite directions; a las introduces the time.

Label preparation:
- Ubicación de la parada — Stop location; original: junto al parque; changed: junto a la tienda
- Vuelta — Turn; original: a la derecha; changed: a la izquierda
- Hora de llegada — Arrival time; original: a las diez; changed: a las once

#### Record the original details

Source: prepared reading

La parada está junto al parque. Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús a la terminal llega a las diez. Compre el boleto en el autobús.

Field key:
- Ubicación de la parada: junto al parque
- Vuelta: a la derecha
- Hora de llegada: a las diez

#### Record the changed source details

Source: prepared message shown as text

La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda. El autobús a la terminal llega a las once. Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.

Field key:
- Ubicación de la parada: junto a la tienda
- Vuelta: a la izquierda
- Hora de llegada: a las once

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A1-travel:confirm

Question purpose: To the left?

Prepared question: ¿A la izquierda?

Partner message: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-travel:correct

Question purpose: To the right?

Prepared question: ¿A la derecha?

Partner message: Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided prepared-pattern feedback

Local pattern recognition supports revision; unmatched wording can be valid. No grammar, pronunciation or proficiency score is assigned. Speaking feedback checks optional notes typed after a real recording and replay, not the audio itself.

#### A1-travel.writing

- Locate the stop
  - Prepared example: La parada está junto al parque.
- Give a direction
  - Prepared example: Siga derecho.
- Include a bus time or ticket detail
  - Prepared example: El autobús llega a las diez.

#### A1-travel.production

- Locate the stop
  - Prepared example: La parada está junto al parque.
- Give a direction
  - Prepared example: Siga derecho.
- Include a bus time or ticket detail
  - Prepared example: El autobús llega a las diez.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask a short question to check a turn, then respond to the clarified direction.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: ¿A la izquierda?
  - Meaning: To the left?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A1-travel.question-confirm.b0681cba0eec.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A1-travel.confirm.4fb1f34fccb1.mp3)

- Checking question: ¿A la derecha?
  - Meaning: To the right?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A1-travel.question-correct.32a16c4979f3.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A1-travel.correct.946e5a1f00ee.mp3)

Partner-detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Own-response goal: Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.

Prepared wording: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

<a id="a1-capstone"></a>
## A1 · Two useful messages

Unit reference: A1-capstone

### Learning goal

Write two short messages: one about the purchase and one about reaching the stop. Keep the two requests separate.

Builds on: A1-travel

### Pattern explanation

Give one useful detail at a time. A polite request can close a brief practical message.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- tienda - shop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-0.1e2490a845d8.mp3)
- manzanas - apples [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-1.6ccf0f8e0360.mp3)
- dos - two [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-2.367fa57b38c5.mp3)
- treinta - thirty [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.word-3.07189528f84e.mp3)
- parada - bus stop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-0.91036fe2da47.mp3)
- autobús - bus [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-1.55c73da44908.mp3)
- derecho - straight ahead [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-2.609b935f9e54.mp3)
- derecha - right (direction) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.word-3.3be32014e5b3.mp3)

### Additional source language

- bien — well / okay, in está bien
  - Está bien.
- compro — I buy / am buying
  - En la tienda compro dos manzanas y pan.
- desea — you would like / want, polite usted
  - ¿Qué desea?
- días — days, in the greeting buenos días
  - ¡Buenos días!
- está — is, in está bien: that is okay
  - Está bien.
- o — or
  - ¿Va a pagar con tarjeta o en efectivo?
- tres — three
  - Quisiera tres manzanas.
- y — and
  - Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha.
- al — a + el: to / by the, in junto al parque
  - La parada está junto al parque.
- boletos — tickets
  - ¿Cuántos boletos quiere?
- comprar — buy
  - Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.
- después — then / afterwards
  - Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda.
- ir — go
  - ¿Adónde quiere ir?
- junto — part of junto a: next to
  - La parada está junto al parque.
- parque — park
  - La parada está junto al parque.
- quiere — you want, polite usted
  - ¿Adónde quiere ir?
- Escribe la hora, por favor. ¡Gracias! — Please write the time. Thank you!
  - Escribe la hora, por favor. ¡Gracias!

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- tienda — shop
- manzanas — apples
- dos manzanas — two apples
- treinta pesos — three euros / thirty pesos
- parada — bus stop
- terminal — station / bus terminal
- Siga derecho. — Go straight.
- Escribe la hora, por favor. ¡Gracias! — Please write the time. Thank you!

### Teaching notes

- Revisit the language and source guides from both preceding cases.
- Give one useful detail at a time. A polite request can close a brief practical message.
- Plan for the stated reader. Use the worked model to study purpose, then supply your own response rather than copying it.

### Reading source

En la tienda compro dos manzanas y pan. Las manzanas cuestan treinta pesos. Voy a pagar con tarjeta. Gracias, ¡hasta luego!

La parada está junto al parque. Siga derecho y dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús a la terminal llega a las diez. Compre el boleto en el autobús.

English guide:
First written source: At the shop I buy two apples and bread. The apples cost three euros / thirty pesos. I will pay by card. Thank you, goodbye!

Second written source: The stop is beside the park. Go straight and turn right. The bus to the terminal arrives at ten. Buy the ticket on the bus.

A useful synthesis pattern

Escribe la hora, por favor. ¡Gracias!

Please write the time. Thank you!

### Listening source

¡Buenos días! Quisiera tres manzanas. ¿Cuánto cuestan? ¿Cuarenta pesos? Está bien. Voy a pagar en efectivo. ¡Gracias!

La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda. El autobús a la terminal llega a las once. Puede comprar un boleto en el autobús.

English guide:
First spoken source: The customer asks for three apples, confirms a price of four euros / forty pesos, and pays in cash.

Second spoken source: This stop is beside the shop. Go straight, then turn left. The terminal bus arrives at eleven. A ticket can be bought on the bus.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-capstone.listen.3dfcf68db1a1.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. First written source: How many apples are bought?
   - Two **[key]**
   - Three
   - Four
2. Second written source: What is beside the stop?
   - The park **[key]**
   - The shop
   - The library
3. Second written source: When does the bus arrive?
   - Eleven
   - Nine
   - Ten **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. First spoken source: How many apples are requested?
   - Two
   - Three **[key]**
   - Four
2. Second spoken source: Which turn is given in the recording?
   - Right
   - Left **[key]**
   - No turn
3. Second spoken source: What time is mentioned?
   - Ten
   - Twelve
   - Eleven **[key]**

### Spoken production

Write two short messages: one about the purchase and one about reaching the stop. Keep the two requests separate.

- Give the useful purchase details.
- Give the useful travel details.
- Close politely or request a useful detail.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¡Buenos días! ¿Qué desea?
   - Meaning: Hello! What would you like?
   - Response goal: Request an item and give a quantity.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-shopping.turn-0.a223a255f704.mp3)
2. ¿Adónde quiere ir?
   - Meaning: Where would you like to go?
   - Response goal: State a simple destination.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.turn-0.6838c467fc51.mp3)
3. ¿Cuántos boletos quiere?
   - Meaning: How many tickets would you like?
   - Response goal: Request a quantity and close politely.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A1-travel.turn-2.3df40ddc2e13.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Explain the two cases to the specified reader. Preserve the differences between the written and spoken versions, and between the two situations. Do not invent a shared person, date, budget or decision.

### Writing assignments

#### Two useful messages · written response

Reader: A friend buying something and finding a bus stop

Write two short messages: one about the purchase and one about reaching the stop. Keep the two requests separate.

Suggested length: 20–65 words.

- Give the useful purchase details.
- Give the useful travel details.
- Close politely or request a useful detail.


##### Project-specific preparation

Study how the two cases remain separate and how the final sentence serves the reader. Plan, draft and revise your own response. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR threshold.

Worked model:
Dos manzanas. Quisiera pan. Gracias, ¡hasta luego!

La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho. Dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús llega a las once.

Escribe la hora, por favor. ¡Gracias!

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Give the useful purchase details.
  - Dos manzanas. Quisiera pan. Gracias, ¡hasta luego!
  - The first case keeps its own details and qualifications. These model details come from the prepared unit.
- Give the useful travel details.
  - La parada está junto a la tienda. Siga derecho. Dé vuelta a la derecha. El autobús llega a las once.
  - The second case is presented separately, so its people, conditions and decisions do not silently replace those of the first.
- Close politely or request a useful detail.
  - Escribe la hora, por favor. ¡Gracias!
  - Give one useful detail at a time. A polite request can close a brief practical message.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A1-shopping:confirm

Question purpose: Forty pesos?

Prepared question: ¿Cuarenta pesos?

Partner message: Cuarenta pesos.

Detail check: Which total did your partner clarify?

Key: Forty pesos

Optional response frame: Cuarenta pesos.

Response goals:
- Restate the price, keeping the amount and currency distinct from the item quantity.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-shopping:correct

Question purpose: Thirty pesos?

Prepared question: ¿Treinta pesos?

Partner message: Cuarenta pesos. Voy a pagar en efectivo.

Detail check: Which total did your partner clarify?

Key: Forty pesos

Optional response frame: Cuarenta pesos.

Response goals:
- Restate the price, keeping the amount and currency distinct from the item quantity.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-travel:confirm

Question purpose: To the left?

Prepared question: ¿A la izquierda?

Partner message: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-travel:correct

Question purpose: To the right?

Prepared question: ¿A la derecha?

Partner message: Siga derecho y después dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Dé vuelta a la izquierda.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.

<a id="a2"></a>
## A2 · Change an everyday plan

Unit reference: A2

### Learning goal

Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence.

Builds on: A1-capstone

### Pattern explanation

Primero and después order events. Porque introduces the reason. Estaba cansada describes the background state here, while regresé gives the completed return.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- ayer - yesterday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-0.cac30da66706.mp3)
- primero - first [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-1.ea7401933d03.mp3)
- después - then / afterwards [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-2.ae66184cf1c7.mp3)
- cansada - tired (female speaker) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-3.c9a5e211fae7.mp3)
- mañana - tomorrow [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-7.e32b2cbea0e6.mp3)
- viernes - Friday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-5.045fae248e6e.mp3)
- porque - because [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-6.f6674e0b8366.mp3)
- llover - to rain [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-7.574da7579a79.mp3)

### Additional source language

- así — part of así que: so / therefore
  - El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho, así que no puedo llegar a las siete.
- cafetería — café
  - Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
- caminar — walk
  - Podemos caminar y tomar un té.
- cita — appointment / arrangement
  - Te llamo por nuestra cita.
- cuándo — when
  - ¿Cuándo podemos vernos?
- del — de + el: of / from the; in al lado del parque: next to the park
  - Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
- dime — tell me, informal tú instruction
  - Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
- esa — that, feminine singular
  - Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
- hacemos — we do / we will do in this plan
  - ¿Y qué hacemos si llueve?
- hora — hour / time
  - Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
- lado — side, in al lado de: next to
  - Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
- llegar — arrive / get there
  - El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho, así que no puedo llegar a las siete.
- llueve — it rains / is raining
  - Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
- mí — me, after a in a mí
  - A mí me viene bien a las siete.
- nuestra — our, feminine singular
  - Te llamo por nuestra cita.
- parece — seems; te parece bien asks if that works for you
  - Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
- propongo — I suggest / propose
  - Propongo a las diez, junto al parque.
- que — that / which; also part of tengo que (have to) and así que (so)
  - Mañana no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar.
- si — if / whether; without an accent
  - Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
- sábado — Saturday
  - ¿Podemos vernos el sábado?
- tengo — I have; tengo que trabajar means I have to work
  - Mañana no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar.
- tomar — have / drink, with un té here
  - Podemos caminar y tomar un té.
- trabajar — work
  - Mañana no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar.
- venir — come
  - ¿Por qué no puedes venir el viernes?
- viene — comes; te viene bien means it works for you
  - A mí me viene bien a las siete.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Ayer estuve en el centro. — Yesterday I was downtown.
- Primero fui al parque. — First I went to the park.
- Después me encontré con una amiga. — Then I met a friend.
- Fue divertido, pero empezó a llover. — It was fun, but it started raining.
- Regresé a casa porque estaba cansada. — I went home because I was tired (female speaker).
- Mañana no puedo ir. — I cannot go tomorrow.
- ¿Podemos vernos el viernes? — Can we meet on Friday?
- Gracias por tu comprensión. — Thank you for understanding.

### Teaching notes

- Use simple connectors to show a real relationship: addition, contrast, reason or sequence.
- A practical message needs both the problem and a clear proposed next step.
- For a past event, keep the order easy to follow. You do not need a long sentence to explain a reason.

### Reading source

¡Hola, Ana! Ayer estuve en el centro. Primero fui al parque y después me encontré con una amiga. Fue divertido, pero empezó a llover. Regresé a casa porque estaba cansada.

Mañana no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar. ¿Podemos vernos el viernes? A mí me viene bien a las siete. Dime si esa hora te parece bien. ¡Gracias por tu comprensión!

English guide:
Hello, Lina/Ana! Yesterday I was in town. First I went to the park, then I met a friend. It was fun, but it started raining. I went home because I was tired.

I cannot come tomorrow because I have to work. Could we meet on Friday? Seven works for me. Write and say whether that time works for you. Thank you for understanding!

### Listening source

Hola, Ana. Te llamo por nuestra cita. El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho, así que no puedo llegar a las siete. ¿Podemos vernos el sábado? Propongo a las diez, junto al parque. Podemos caminar y tomar un té. Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque. Dime si te viene bien el sábado. ¡Gracias!

English guide:
The caller must work until eight on Friday and cannot come at seven. They propose Saturday at ten beside the park, for a walk and tea. If it rains, they suggest the café next to the park. They ask the listener to confirm Saturday.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.listen.06eb1d67c860.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. Why does the writer change the plan?
   - She has to work **[key]**
   - She dislikes her friend
2. What should the recipient do?
   - Confirm whether the new time works **[key]**
   - Buy train tickets
3. How does the writer describe the outing?
   - They stayed home all day
   - It was fun before the rain began **[key]**
   - They disliked meeting their friend

### Listening task key

1. Why does the Friday plan change?
   - The caller is ill
   - The caller has to work **[key]**
   - The park is closed
2. What replacement is suggested?
   - Friday at ten
   - Saturday at eight
   - Saturday at ten **[key]**
3. What happens if it rains?
   - They meet in the nearby café **[key]**
   - They cancel every plan
   - They meet at the workplace

### Spoken production

Describe a real or invented day out, explain a practical problem and suggest a new arrangement.

- Put events in a clear order.
- Explain why the original plan changed.
- Suggest a day and time, and ask for confirmation.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Por qué no puedes venir el viernes?
   - Meaning: Why can you not come on Friday?
   - Response goal: Explain a practical reason.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.turn-0.7eaa970e8920.mp3)
2. ¿Cuándo podemos vernos?
   - Meaning: When can we meet?
   - Response goal: Suggest another day and time.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.turn-1.f3606e0263d9.mp3)
3. ¿Y qué hacemos si llueve?
   - Meaning: And what will we do if it rains?
   - Response goal: Offer a practical alternative.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.turn-2.e90e30fce633.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Leave a short voice message explaining the two different changed plans in the reading and listening. Do not confuse Friday’s written proposal with Saturday’s spoken proposal.

### Writing assignments

#### Tell a friend about a day out

Reader: A friend

Describe a recent real or invented outing.

Suggested length: 20–70 words.

- Put the events in order.
- Say what you liked or disliked.
- Explain one reason.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Ayer estuve en el centro. Primero fui al parque y después me encontré con una amiga. Fue divertido, pero empezó a llover. Regresé a casa porque estaba cansada.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Put the events in order
  - Ayer estuve en el centro. Primero fui al parque y después me encontré con una amiga.
  - The time and sequence words show what happened first and next.
- Say what you liked or disliked
  - Fue divertido, pero empezó a llover.
  - This gives a reaction and a contrasting complication.
- Explain one reason
  - Regresé a casa porque estaba cansada.
  - Nes / porque links the action to its reason, rather than just listing another event.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### Change an arrangement

Reader: A friend or classmate

You cannot attend an agreed meeting. Send a useful message.

Suggested length: 20–70 words.

- Explain why you cannot attend.
- Suggest another day or time.
- Ask for confirmation.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
El viernes no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar. ¿Podemos vernos el sábado a las diez junto al parque? Dime si te viene bien el sábado.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Explain why you cannot attend
  - El viernes no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar.
  - The day, problem and reason are connected in one practical explanation.
- Propose another arrangement
  - ¿Podemos vernos el sábado a las diez junto al parque?
  - A useful alternative gives a day, time and place; it is a proposal, not a confirmed booking.
- Ask for confirmation
  - Dime si te viene bien el sábado.
  - This asks the other person to confirm whether the replacement works.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### Recommend a familiar place

Reader: A visitor

Describe a place you know or invent a simple place.

Suggested length: 20–70 words.

- Say what someone can do there.
- Give a reason to visit.
- Mention a practical detail or limitation.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
En la cafetería que está al lado del parque podemos tomar un té. Propongo vernos junto al parque porque podemos caminar. A mí me viene bien a las siete.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Say what someone can do there
  - En la cafetería que está al lado del parque podemos tomar un té.
  - This identifies the place and a simple activity available there.
- Give a reason to visit
  - Propongo vernos junto al parque porque podemos caminar.
  - The reason makes the proposal useful for the visitor instead of only naming a place.
- Give a practical detail
  - A mí me viene bien a las siete.
  - A convenient meeting time is a practical detail. It is not a claim about opening hours.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A message proposing another arrangement

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### saturday-eight-rain

El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho, así que no puedo llegar a las siete. ¿Podemos vernos el sábado a las ocho junto al parque? Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque. Dime si te viene bien esa hora.

- Why is Friday at seven not possible?
  - Model detail: El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho, así que no puedo llegar a las siete.
  - The writer works until eight **[key]**
  - The park closes at seven
  - The friend has already cancelled Saturday
  - Explanation: The reason is the writer’s work, not a closure or a reply from the friend.
- Which replacement is proposed in this message?
  - Model detail: ¿Podemos vernos el sábado a las ocho junto al parque?
  - Saturday at ten
  - Friday at eight
  - Saturday at eight **[key]**
  - Explanation: Read this proposal rather than carrying over the ten o’clock time from the prepared message.
- What changes if it rains?
  - Model detail: Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
  - The meeting is automatically cancelled
  - They meet in the café beside the park **[key]**
  - The meeting moves back to Friday
  - Explanation: The condition changes the meeting place; it does not cancel the arrangement or change its day.

#### friday-ten-reply

Mañana no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar. ¿Podemos vernos el viernes a las diez? Dime si esa hora te parece bien. Si llueve, nos vemos en una cafetería.

- What explains the proposed change?
  - Model detail: Mañana no puedo ir porque tengo que trabajar.
  - The café has confirmed a booking
  - The writer has to work tomorrow **[key]**
  - The friend refuses every meeting
  - Explanation: Porque introduces the reason for not coming tomorrow.
- What should the reader do next?
  - Model detail: Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
  - Reply whether the proposed time works **[key]**
  - Assume ten has already been agreed
  - Ask the writer to work until ten
  - Explanation: The writer requests a response about the proposed time.
- What can you conclude about Friday at ten?
  - Model detail: ¿Podemos vernos el viernes a las diez? Dime si esa hora te parece bien.
  - Both people have already confirmed it
  - It has been cancelled because of rain
  - It is proposed, with a reply still requested **[key]**
  - Explanation: A proposal and request for a reply do not establish that the other person has accepted.

### Controlled listening transfer

A voice message about an arrangement or a day out

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### friday-ten-park

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2.friday-ten-park.af53182888cd.mp3)

Hola, Ana. Te llamo por nuestra cita. El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho. Propongo a las diez, junto al parque. Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque. Dime si te viene bien esa hora. Gracias por tu comprensión.

- Which meeting time does the speaker propose?
  - Model detail: Propongo a las diez, junto al parque.
  - Eight
  - Ten **[key]**
  - Seven
  - Explanation: Eight is when work ends. Ten is the proposed meeting time.
- What happens if it rains?
  - Model detail: Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
  - The friend must work later
  - The meeting is automatically cancelled
  - They meet in the café beside the park **[key]**
  - Explanation: The rain condition changes the place, not the time or the need to reply.
- What does the speaker still need from the listener?
  - Model detail: Dime si te viene bien esa hora.
  - A reply about whether the time works **[key]**
  - Proof that the park is open
  - A confirmation that the meeting already happened
  - Explanation: The message asks for a reply; it does not establish that the listener has accepted.

#### outing-tired-home

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2.outing-tired-home.40ac93dcadd4.mp3)

¡Hola! Ayer estuve en el centro. Primero fui al parque. Después me encontré con una amiga. Fue divertido, pero empezó a llover. Regresé a casa porque estaba cansada. ¿Podemos vernos el sábado? Propongo a las siete, junto al parque.

- What happened before meeting the friend?
  - Model detail: Primero fui al parque. Después me encontré con una amiga.
  - The speaker went home
  - The speaker went to the park **[key]**
  - The speaker worked until eight
  - Explanation: Primero and después give the order of the two events.
- What reason does the speaker give for going home?
  - Model detail: Regresé a casa porque estaba cansada.
  - The friend cancelled Saturday
  - The café was closed
  - The speaker was tired **[key]**
  - Explanation: Rain is mentioned, but the stated reason for returning home is tiredness.
- Which new arrangement is proposed?
  - Model detail: ¿Podemos vernos el sábado? Propongo a las siete, junto al parque.
  - Saturday at seven **[key]**
  - Friday at ten
  - Saturday at eight
  - Explanation: The proposed Saturday meeting is separate from the account of yesterday.

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A2:time

Question purpose: When can we meet?

Prepared question: ¿Cuándo podemos vernos?

Partner message: Propongo a las diez, junto al parque. Dime si te viene bien el sábado.

Detail check: What arrangement does your partner propose?

Key: Saturday at ten near the park

Optional response frame: Propongo a las diez, junto al parque. Dime si te viene bien el sábado.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge Saturday at ten and say whether it works for you, or propose a prepared alternative.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2:rain

Question purpose: And what will we do if it rains?

Prepared question: ¿Y qué hacemos si llueve?

Partner message: Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.

Detail check: What does rain change?

Key: The meeting place becomes the café beside the park

Optional response frame: Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge the rain plan and restate the café location without changing the day or time.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask about the replacement time or the rain condition, then make a useful response.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: ¿Cuándo podemos vernos?
  - Meaning: When can we meet?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2.question-time.f3606e0263d9.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Propongo a las diez, junto al parque. Dime si te viene bien el sábado.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2.time.e39c41da31f1.mp3)

- Checking question: ¿Y qué hacemos si llueve?
  - Meaning: And what will we do if it rains?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2.question-rain.e90e30fce633.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2.rain.c891616558cc.mp3)

B1–C2 question frames are optional and their use is saved. Intention selection drives the partner; speech is never interpreted automatically.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: time

What arrangement does your partner propose?

Key: Saturday at ten near the park

Own-response goal: Acknowledge Saturday at ten and say whether it works for you, or propose a prepared alternative.

Prepared wording: Propongo a las diez, junto al parque. Dime si te viene bien el sábado.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: rain

What does rain change?

Key: The meeting place becomes the café beside the park

Own-response goal: Acknowledge the rain plan and restate the café location without changing the day or time.

Prepared wording: Si llueve, nos vemos en la cafetería que está al lado del parque.

<a id="a2-services"></a>
## A2 · Arrange and change an appointment

Unit reference: A2-services

### Learning goal

Follow opening times, explain a booking problem and agree a replacement.

Builds on: A2

### Pattern explanation

Quisiera makes a polite request; puedo gives availability. Ese horario no me viene bien rejects that time, not every possible appointment. Confirme addresses the other person politely.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- cita - appointment [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-0.ced5b5cee531.mp3)
- documentos - documents [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-1.12f85c87e40d.mp3)
- martes - Tuesday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-2.f0c5a6256e4c.mp3)
- viernes - Friday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-5.045fae248e6e.mp3)
- antes - before / earlier [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-4.8bc0712fd02d.mp3)
- confirmar - to confirm [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-5.483f551c336e.mp3)
- horario - time slot / schedule [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-6.e754c9ba7590.mp3)
- jueves - Thursday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-7.2d462c464c3b.mp3)

### Additional source language

- ayuda — help
  - Gracias por su ayuda.
- cambiarla — change it, referring to the appointment
  - Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres.
- confirmación — confirmation
  - ¿Cómo desea recibir la confirmación?
- confirmen — confirm, polite plural instruction
  - Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
- debe — you must / should, polite usted
  - Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.
- día — day
  - ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?
- escríbanos — write to us, polite usted instruction
  - Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos.
- espacio — available slot / space in the schedule
  - El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde.
- hay — there is / there are
  - Esa mañana no hay lugar.
- le — to / for you, polite usted in le viene bien
  - Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos.
- llegue — arrive, polite usted instruction
