  - Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.
- lugar — place / room in the schedule
  - Esa mañana no hay lugar.
- mensaje — message
  - Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
- minutos — minutes
  - Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.
- nuevo — new, masculine singular
  - Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.
- para — for / in order to
  - Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez.
- poco — a little, in un poco antes
  - Voy a llevar los documentos y llegar un poco antes.
- recibir — receive
  - ¿Cómo desea recibir la confirmación?
- revisar — review / check
  - Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez.
- sirve — works / is suitable, in le sirve
  - ¿Le sirve por la tarde?
- su — your, polite usted; or their, according to context
  - Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez.
- tenemos — we have
  - El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde.
- traiga — bring, polite usted instruction
  - Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Quisiera hacer una cita. — I would like to make an appointment.
- ¿Tiene algún horario disponible el martes? — Do you have an available time on Tuesday?
- Puedo ir por la tarde. — I can come in the afternoon.
- Lo siento, ese horario no me viene bien. — Unfortunately, that time does not work for me.
- Quisiera cambiar la hora de la cita. — I would like to change the appointment time.
- ¿Se puede el viernes? — Is Friday possible?
- Por favor, confirme el horario. — Please confirm the time.
- Voy a llevar los documentos. — I will bring the documents.

### Teaching notes

- A booking exchange needs the day, time and any required action.
- Use a reason when declining a time, then offer a possible alternative.
- Keep the old booking separate from the new one. “Change” does not mean “cancel without replacing”.

### Reading source

Buenos días. Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez. Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos. Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos. El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde. Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.

English guide:
The document appointment is Tuesday at ten. Arrive ten minutes early and bring documents. Friday at three in the afternoon is available as a replacement, which must be confirmed before Thursday.

### Listening source

Buenos días, llamo por mi cita. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos y llegar un poco antes. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Gracias por su ayuda.

English guide:
The caller cannot attend Tuesday morning because of work. They ask for Friday at three, will bring documents and arrive early, and request confirmation by message.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.listen.ac2f903495be.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. What is the original appointment?
   - Tuesday at ten **[key]**
   - Friday at ten
   - Thursday at three
2. What should the visitor bring?
   - A ticket
   - Documents **[key]**
   - Food
3. When must a replacement be confirmed?
   - After Friday
   - On Saturday
   - Before Thursday **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. Why does the caller change the booking?
   - They are travelling
   - They have to work **[key]**
   - They lost the address
2. Which time do they request?
   - Friday at three **[key]**
   - Tuesday at three
   - Friday at ten
3. How do they request confirmation?
   - By letter
   - Only in person
   - By message **[key]**

### Spoken production

Explain an appointment change and propose a replacement.

- State the original appointment.
- Explain why it needs to change.
- Give a replacement and request confirmation.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?
   - Meaning: Which day would you like an appointment?
   - Response goal: Request a day and a time of day.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.turn-0.04022d0f7991.mp3)
2. Esa mañana no hay lugar. ¿Le sirve por la tarde?
   - Meaning: That morning is full. Would the afternoon work?
   - Response goal: Accept the afternoon or propose another day with a reason.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.turn-1.96b39b81fd38.mp3)
3. ¿Cómo desea recibir la confirmación?
   - Meaning: How would you like confirmation?
   - Response goal: Request confirmation and mention what you will bring.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.turn-2.c023b705f175.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Explain the office’s instructions and the caller’s requested change to someone helping organise the visit.

### Writing assignments

#### Change a booking

Reader: A service office

Write a polite message changing an invented appointment.

Suggested length: 20–85 words.

- Identify the original booking.
- Explain the problem and propose an alternative.
- Request confirmation and acknowledge any required documents.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Mi cita es el martes a las diez. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Identify the original booking
  - Mi cita es el martes a las diez.
  - This lets the office identify the appointment that needs changing.
- Explain the problem and propose a replacement
  - El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres.
  - The replacement is a request. Keep it separate from the original appointment.
- Request confirmation and acknowledge the documents
  - Voy a llevar los documentos. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
  - The reader knows what you will bring and how to confirm the new arrangement.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A service appointment message or notice

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### request-tuesday-ten

Mi cita es el viernes a las tres de la tarde. El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al martes a las diez. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.

- Which new appointment is being requested?
  - Model detail: Quisiera cambiarla al martes a las diez.
  - Friday at three
  - Tuesday at ten **[key]**
  - Friday at eight
  - Explanation: Friday at three identifies the original appointment; Tuesday at ten is the requested replacement.
- Why does the original appointment not work?
  - Model detail: El viernes tengo que trabajar hasta las ocho y no puedo ir.
  - The writer is working until eight on Friday **[key]**
  - The office refuses to accept documents
  - The new appointment has already happened
  - Explanation: The work commitment conflicts with the original Friday appointment.
- What is the status of the new time?
  - Model detail: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
  - It is guaranteed by the writer’s message
  - It has been refused by the office
  - Confirmation by message is still requested **[key]**
  - Explanation: Requesting confirmation is different from reporting that confirmation was received.

#### friday-notice

Su cita es el viernes a las tres de la tarde. Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos. Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos. Debe confirmar el horario antes del jueves.

- What should the visitor do for the appointment?
  - Model detail: Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.
  - Arrive exactly ten minutes late without documents
  - Arrive ten minutes early and bring documents **[key]**
  - Bring documents only if the appointment is cancelled
  - Explanation: The instruction contains two actions; neither is conditional on cancellation.
- When should the visitor write to the office?
  - Model detail: Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos.
  - If the proposed time does not work **[key]**
  - Only after missing the appointment
  - Only if the office has lost the documents
  - Explanation: Si introduces the condition for contacting the office.
- Which confirmation deadline is given?
  - Model detail: Debe confirmar el horario antes del jueves.
  - After Friday
  - On Tuesday
  - Before Thursday **[key]**
  - Explanation: Antes means before; the appointment itself is on Friday.

### Controlled listening transfer

An appointment request or a service office message

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### request-friday-ten

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2-services.request-friday-ten.d6823747e6d2.mp3)

Buenos días, llamo por mi cita. Mi cita es el martes a las diez. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las diez. Voy a llevar los documentos. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.

- Which replacement does the caller request?
  - Model detail: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las diez.
  - Tuesday at ten
  - Friday at three
  - Friday at ten **[key]**
  - Explanation: Tuesday at ten is the old appointment. The request moves it to Friday at ten.
- Why can the caller not attend the original appointment?
  - Model detail: El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir.
  - The caller has to work on Tuesday morning **[key]**
  - The documents have already been refused
  - The office is closed all day on Friday
  - Explanation: The caller gives a work conflict as the reason.
- How does the caller want confirmation?
  - Model detail: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
  - Through a call to a client
  - By message **[key]**
  - By arriving without a reply
  - Explanation: Confirmation by message is requested; this call itself does not confirm availability.

#### office-tuesday-three

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2-services.office-tuesday-three.89794be5e5ce.mp3)

Buenos días. Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las tres de la tarde. Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos. Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos. El viernes tenemos un espacio a las diez. Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.

- When is the existing appointment?
  - Model detail: Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las tres de la tarde.
  - Friday at ten
  - Tuesday at three in the afternoon **[key]**
  - Tuesday at ten
  - Explanation: Tuesday afternoon is the existing appointment; Friday is an alternative.
- Which alternative does the office offer?
  - Model detail: El viernes tenemos un espacio a las diez.
  - Friday at ten **[key]**
  - Friday at three
  - Tuesday at eight
  - Explanation: An available alternative is offered; it is not automatically a confirmed replacement.
- What should the listener do if the existing time does not work?
  - Model detail: Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos.
  - Wait until after missing the appointment
  - Assume the Friday appointment is already booked
  - Write to the office **[key]**
  - Explanation: The office asks the listener to make contact if the time is unsuitable.

### Required practical table reading

A table gives meaning through both its headings and the position of each cell. Find the requested column before following its row.
The row labels distinguish original details from the changed or requested details. A proposed replacement is not proof that a real booking has been confirmed.
The second task turns the table: field labels run down the side. Compare the table with the displayed message before selecting an answer.
- Original details: Día de la cita = el martes; Hora de la cita = a las diez; Documentos necesarios = los documentos
- Changed / requested details: Día de la cita = el viernes; Hora de la cita = a las tres; Documentos necesarios = los documentos

#### Read across the rows

Layout: rows

Displayed comparison source:
Buenos días. Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez. Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos. Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos. El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde. Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.

| Source details | Día de la cita | Hora de la cita | Documentos necesarios |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Original details | el martes | a las diez | los documentos |
| Changed / requested details | el viernes | a las tres | los documentos |

- In the original details, what belongs under Día de la cita?
  - el martes **[key]**
  - el viernes
  - a las diez
  - los documentos
  - Explanation: Día de la cita means appointment day. Follow that column to the original details row.
- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Documentos necesarios?
  - los documentos **[key]**
  - el martes
  - a las diez
  - Explanation: Documentos necesarios means required documents. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- Compare the two rows. Which details change?
  - Appointment day + Appointment time **[key]**
  - Appointment day
  - Appointment time
  - Required documents
  - None of the details
  - Explanation: Changed fields: Appointment day + Appointment time. Unchanged: Required documents.

#### Compare a message with the table

Layout: columns

Displayed comparison source:
Buenos días, llamo por mi cita. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos y llegar un poco antes. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Gracias por su ayuda.

| Field | Original details | Changed / requested details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Día de la cita | el martes | el viernes |
| Hora de la cita | a las diez | a las tres |
| Documentos necesarios | los documentos | los documentos |

- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Día de la cita?
  - el viernes **[key]**
  - el martes
  - a las diez
  - los documentos
  - Explanation: Día de la cita means appointment day. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- In the original details, what belongs under Hora de la cita?
  - a las diez **[key]**
  - a las tres
  - el martes
  - los documentos
  - Explanation: Hora de la cita means appointment time. Follow that column to the original details row.
- Which table column matches the displayed changed / requested message?
  - Original details
  - Changed / requested details **[key]**
  - Explanation: Use the changed / requested details column. The other column records the original source.

### Required practical forms

The labels and field formats are explicitly taught. Both prepared-source forms are required; detail checking is not an open-writing accuracy grade.
El martes / el viernes identify the day. Keep a las with the time. Los documentos names what to bring; the source gives the afternoon context for the new appointment.

Label preparation:
- Día de la cita — Appointment day; original: el martes; changed: el viernes
- Hora de la cita — Appointment time; original: a las diez; changed: a las tres
- Documentos necesarios — Required documents; original: los documentos; changed: los documentos

#### Record the original details

Source: prepared reading

Buenos días. Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez. Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos. Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos. El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde. Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.

Field key:
- Día de la cita: el martes
- Hora de la cita: a las diez
- Documentos necesarios: los documentos

#### Record the requested replacement

Source: prepared message shown as text

Buenos días, llamo por mi cita. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos y llegar un poco antes. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Gracias por su ayuda.

Field key:
- Día de la cita: el viernes
- Hora de la cita: a las tres
- Documentos necesarios: los documentos

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A2-services:day

Question purpose: Which day would you like an appointment?

Prepared question: ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?

Partner message: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Por favor, confirme el horario.

Detail check: Which appointment is requested?

Key: Friday at three

Optional response frame: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Por favor, confirme el horario.

Response goals:
- Restate the requested day and time, and distinguish the request from a confirmed appointment.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2-services:confirmation

Question purpose: How would you like confirmation?

Prepared question: ¿Cómo desea recibir la confirmación?

Partner message: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.

Detail check: How does the caller want confirmation?

Key: By a message

Optional response frame: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge the requested confirmation by message and the documents, without claiming confirmation has already arrived.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask the caller which appointment they want or how they want confirmation.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?
  - Meaning: Which day would you like an appointment?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-services.question-day.04022d0f7991.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Por favor, confirme el horario.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-services.day.ec1033b48802.mp3)

- Checking question: ¿Cómo desea recibir la confirmación?
  - Meaning: How would you like confirmation?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-services.question-confirmation.c023b705f175.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-services.confirmation.bcc141be6288.mp3)

B1–C2 question frames are optional and their use is saved. Intention selection drives the partner; speech is never interpreted automatically.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: day

Which appointment is requested?

Key: Friday at three

Own-response goal: Restate the requested day and time, and distinguish the request from a confirmed appointment.

Prepared wording: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Por favor, confirme el horario.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: confirmation

How does the caller want confirmation?

Key: By a message

Own-response goal: Acknowledge the requested confirmation by message and the documents, without claiming confirmation has already arrived.

Prepared wording: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.

<a id="a2-work"></a>
## A2 · Plan a working day

Unit reference: A2-work

### Learning goal

Compare routines, understand a changed schedule and explain practical responsibilities.

Builds on: A2-services

### Pattern explanation

Empiezo and reviso describe the routine. Voy a llamar and voy a estar mark plans. A las doce y media means half past twelve.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- trabajo - work [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-0.9758ba92f3a2.mp3)
- mensajes - messages [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-1.8338db04358d.mp3)
- reunión - meeting [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-2.53e56db23654.mp3)
- cliente - client [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-3.6fc54ed32c31.mp3)
- casa - home [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A1.word-4.ed4d103c57b5.mp3)
- oficina - office [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-5.897f73e7552d.mp3)
- tareas - tasks [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-6.ecb7bc104a3a.mp3)
- ayuda - help [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-7.e66807f372b5.mp3)

### Additional source language

- avísame — let me know, informal tú instruction
  - Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
- clientes — clients
  - Después llamo a los clientes.
- conectarme — connect / join online, referring to myself
  - Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde.
- empieza — starts
  - Mañana la reunión empieza una hora más tarde.
- equipo — team
  - Primero reviso los mensajes y a las diez me reúno con el equipo.
- espero — I am waiting for, in espero a un técnico
  - Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
- internet — internet
  - Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde.
- juntos — together
  - Por la tarde llamamos juntos a un cliente nuevo.
- lista — list
  - Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor.
- llamamos — we call / will call, in this plan
  - Por la tarde llamamos juntos a un cliente nuevo.
- normalmente — usually / normally
  - Normalmente empiezo a trabajar a las nueve en la oficina.
- prepara — prepare, informal tú instruction
  - Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor.
- reúno — part of me reúno: I meet / get together
  - Primero reviso los mensajes y a las diez me reúno con el equipo.
- ser — be, in va a ser: is going to be
  - Hola, mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.
- terminas — you finish, informal tú
  - Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
- técnico — technician / repair worker
  - Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
- yo — I
  - Yo voy a revisar los documentos.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. — I start work at nine.
- Primero reviso los mensajes. — First I check messages.
- Después de la reunión voy a llamar al cliente. — After the meeting I will call the client.
- Como a las doce y media. — I have lunch at half past twelve.
- Hoy trabajo desde casa. — Today I work from home.
- Mañana voy a estar en la oficina. — Tomorrow I will be at the office.
- Necesito ayuda. — I need help.
- Podemos repartir las tareas. — We can divide the tasks.

### Teaching notes

- Time expressions and sequence words show how a routine is organised.
- A temporary change today does not replace every part of someone’s normal routine.
- When sharing work, say who will do what and when it is needed.

### Reading source

Normalmente empiezo a trabajar a las nueve en la oficina. Primero reviso los mensajes y a las diez me reúno con el equipo. Después llamo a los clientes. Como a las doce y media. Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde.

English guide:
Normally the writer starts at nine in the office, checks messages, meets the team at ten and then calls clients. Lunch is at twelve thirty. Today they work at home waiting for a technician, attend online and move client calls to the afternoon.

### Listening source

Hola, mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la tarde llamamos juntos a un cliente nuevo. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once. Podemos repartir el trabajo.

English guide:
Tomorrow’s meeting moves from ten to eleven. The listener should prepare a client list; the speaker will check documents. They will call a new client together in the afternoon. If the list will not be ready, the listener should say so before eleven.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.listen.13e935402ee0.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. Why is the writer at home today?
   - Waiting for a technician **[key]**
   - The office has closed permanently
   - They are on holiday
2. What stays in the morning?
   - The client calls
   - The team meeting **[key]**
   - Lunch
3. When is the normal lunch break?
   - At eleven
   - At one thirty
   - At twelve thirty **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. What is the new meeting time?
   - Ten
   - Eleven **[key]**
   - Twelve
2. Who checks the documents?
   - The speaker **[key]**
   - The new client
   - The listener alone
3. What should happen if the list is not ready?
   - Cancel every call
   - Say nothing until afternoon
   - Notify the speaker before eleven **[key]**

### Spoken production

Describe a routine and explain how a change affects it.

- Give a clear normal sequence.
- Distinguish the temporary change.
- Explain who will do the necessary tasks.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Cómo es normalmente su mañana de trabajo?
   - Meaning: What is your normal working morning like?
   - Response goal: Describe two activities in order.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.turn-0.13d3e1800158.mp3)
2. Mañana la reunión empieza una hora más tarde. ¿Le viene bien?
   - Meaning: Tomorrow the meeting starts an hour later. Does that work?
   - Response goal: Respond and explain any practical effect on your day.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.turn-1.6c5ad5575938.mp3)
3. ¿Cómo podemos repartir las tareas?
   - Meaning: How can we divide the tasks?
   - Response goal: Offer to do one task and ask the partner to do another.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.turn-2.da716c936852.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Brief a colleague on which timetable details concern today’s home-working arrangement and which concern tomorrow’s changed meeting.

### Writing assignments

#### A useful team update

Reader: Your team

Write an update explaining a temporary schedule change and how work will be shared.

Suggested length: 25–90 words.

- State the change and a reason.
- Give clear times or sequence.
- Assign responsibilities and say when help is needed.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- State the change and its reason
  - Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
  - The temporary work-location change is linked to its reason.
- Give times or sequence
  - Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet.
  - The team gets a start time and knows how you will join the meeting.
- Assign work and explain when help is needed
  - Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
  - Each task has an owner. The condition and deadline explain when the colleague should ask for help.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A practical update to a colleague

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### remote-ten-deadline

Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet. Nuestra reunión va a ser a las diez, no a las once. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

- Why is the writer working from home?
  - Model detail: Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
  - The writer is waiting for a repair worker **[key]**
  - The team meeting has been cancelled
  - The clients have moved to another office
  - Explanation: The stated reason is waiting for the repair worker; joining online does not mean the meeting is cancelled.
- Which meeting time does this update give?
  - Model detail: Nuestra reunión va a ser a las diez, no a las once.
  - Eleven
  - Nine
  - Ten **[key]**
  - Explanation: The update contrasts ten with eleven. Do not carry over the time from the prepared recording.
- What should the colleague do if the list will not be ready?
  - Model detail: Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
  - Assume the writer has prepared it
  - Let the writer know before eleven **[key]**
  - Call every client before nine
  - Explanation: The conditional instruction gives a communication step and a time for it.

#### office-after-meeting

Mañana voy a estar en la oficina. Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. Después de la reunión voy a llamar al cliente. Prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Podemos repartir las tareas.

- When will the writer call the client?
  - Model detail: Después de la reunión voy a llamar al cliente.
  - Before starting work
  - After the meeting **[key]**
  - Only after receiving a new appointment
  - Explanation: Después de la reunión gives the sequence, not a separate clock time.
- Who says they will check the documents?
  - Model detail: Yo voy a revisar los documentos.
  - The writer **[key]**
  - The client
  - The repair worker
  - Explanation: Yo identifies the writer’s responsibility; the list preparation is requested from the colleague.
- What deadline for preparing the list is stated here?
  - Model detail: Prepara la lista de clientes, por favor.
  - It must be ready before eleven
  - It must be ready after lunch
  - No explicit deadline is given in this message **[key]**
  - Explanation: This request gives no deadline. A deadline from another lesson message must not be added to this one.

### Controlled listening transfer

A colleague explains timing, responsibilities or a temporary change

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### office-eleven-call

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2-work.office-eleven-call.87bcda0124fc.mp3)

Hola. Mañana voy a estar en la oficina. Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. Nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez. Después de la reunión voy a llamar al cliente. Prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Podemos repartir las tareas.

- What time is the meeting now?
  - Model detail: Nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.
  - Nine
  - Ten
  - Eleven **[key]**
  - Explanation: Nine is the work start; ten is the meeting time being replaced.
- Who is asked to prepare the client list?
  - Model detail: Prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Yo voy a revisar los documentos.
  - The colleague receiving the message **[key]**
  - The client
  - The repair worker
  - Explanation: The speaker asks the colleague to prepare the list and takes responsibility for the documents.
- When will the speaker call the client?
  - Model detail: Después de la reunión voy a llamar al cliente.
  - Before starting work
  - After the meeting **[key]**
  - Only if the colleague cannot prepare the list
  - Explanation: The call follows the meeting; it is not made conditional on a problem with the list.

#### remote-online-help

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2-work.remote-online-help.05c3eea3a228.mp3)

Hola. Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once. Podemos repartir el trabajo.

- Why is the speaker at home today?
  - Model detail: Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
  - The team has cancelled work
  - The speaker is waiting for a repair worker **[key]**
  - The clients cannot use the internet
  - Explanation: The temporary location change has a stated reason: waiting for the repair worker.
- How will the speaker take part in the team meeting?
  - Model detail: Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet
  - By joining online **[key]**
  - By cancelling it
  - By sending the client instead
  - Explanation: Working at home does not mean missing or cancelling the meeting.
- What should the listener do if the list will not be ready?
  - Model detail: Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once. Podemos repartir el trabajo.
  - Call the clients before lunch
  - Wait without telling anyone
  - Tell the speaker before eleven so they can share the work **[key]**
  - Explanation: The condition leads to a request for contact and an offer to divide the work.

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A2-work:meeting

Question purpose: Tomorrow the meeting starts an hour later. Does that work?

Prepared question: Mañana la reunión empieza una hora más tarde. ¿Le viene bien?

Partner message: Mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.

Detail check: What time is now proposed?

Key: Eleven

Optional response frame: Mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.

Response goals:
- Restate eleven rather than ten and say whether the new time works for you.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2-work:tasks

Question purpose: How can we divide the tasks?

Prepared question: ¿Cómo podemos repartir las tareas?

Partner message: Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

Detail check: What is the listener asked to do?

Key: Prepare the list and report a problem by the stated deadline

Optional response frame: Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge your list task, keep the speaker’s document task separate, and mention when you would ask for help.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Check the changed meeting time or divide practical work clearly.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: Mañana la reunión empieza una hora más tarde. ¿Le viene bien?
  - Meaning: Tomorrow the meeting starts an hour later. Does that work?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-work.question-meeting.6c5ad5575938.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-work.meeting.8ba4bf178ee4.mp3)

- Checking question: ¿Cómo podemos repartir las tareas?
  - Meaning: How can we divide the tasks?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-work.question-tasks.da716c936852.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-work.tasks.281c4c868eb0.mp3)

B1–C2 question frames are optional and their use is saved. Intention selection drives the partner; speech is never interpreted automatically.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: meeting

What time is now proposed?

Key: Eleven

Own-response goal: Restate eleven rather than ten and say whether the new time works for you.

Prepared wording: Mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: tasks

What is the listener asked to do?

Key: Prepare the list and report a problem by the stated deadline

Own-response goal: Acknowledge your list task, keep the speaker’s document task separate, and mention when you would ask for help.

Prepared wording: Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

<a id="a2-meals"></a>
## A2 · Plan a meal for a group

Unit reference: A2-meals

### Learning goal

Understand preferences, compare a plan with a changed order and make a practical request.

Builds on: A2-work

### Pattern explanation

No como carne gives a preference; sin carne modifies the requested food. Quisiéramos is a request for we. La salsa aparte asks for sauce separately; it does not establish an allergy.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- carne - meat [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-0.ce84bcb09dd4.mp3)
- platillos - dishes [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-1.1fab8292481a.mp3)
- personas - people [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-2.d9f8329877de.mp3)
- salsa - sauce [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-3.491e774335a4.mp3)
- aparte - separately [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-4.0bba1fd1f785.mp3)
- esperar - to wait [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-5.0668e3588440.mp3)
- pedido - order [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-6.a1375583570a.mp3)
- siete y media - seven thirty [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.word-7.2c4047bd01c9.mp3)

### Additional source language

- alguna — any / some, feminine singular
  - ¿Tienen alguna preferencia para los platillos?
- avísennos — let us know, polite plural instruction
  - Si hay que esperar más, avísennos por mensaje.
- cena — dinner
  - El sábado quisiéramos pedir cena para cuatro personas.
- cinco — five
  - Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.
- comen — they eat
  - Dos no comen carne y necesitan platillos sin carne.
- cuántas — how many, feminine plural with personas
  - ¿Cuántas personas vienen en su grupo?
- grupo — group
  - ¿Cuántas personas vienen en su grupo?
- les — to / for you, plural, in les parece bien
  - ¿Les parece bien?
- mantienen — part of se mantienen: remain unchanged
  - Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen.
- necesitan — they need
  - Dos no comen carne y necesitan platillos sin carne.
- número — number / count
  - ¿Podría confirmar el nuevo número de personas y la hora?
- otras — the other ones, feminine plural
  - Las otras dos quieren pollo.
- platos — dishes
  - Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen.
- podría — could you, polite usted in the request
  - ¿Podría confirmar el nuevo número de personas y la hora?
- pollo — chicken
  - Las otras dos quieren pollo.
- preferencia — preference
  - ¿Tienen alguna preferencia para los platillos?
- queriendo — wanting; seguimos queriendo means we still want
  - Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.
- quieren — they want
  - Las otras dos quieren pollo.
- seguimos — we continue / still do, in seguimos queriendo
  - Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.
- sirvan — serve, polite plural instruction
  - Sirvan la salsa aparte para todos, por favor.
- solo — only
  - Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.
- tienen — you have, plural ustedes in the question
  - ¿Tienen alguna preferencia para los platillos?
- todos — everyone / all
  - Sirvan la salsa aparte para todos, por favor.
- vamos — we go; vamos a llegar means we are going to arrive
  - Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.
- vienen — you are coming, plural ustedes in the question
  - ¿Cuántas personas vienen en su grupo?

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- No como carne. — I do not eat meat.
- ¿Tiene platillos sin carne? — Do you have dishes without meat?
- Quisiéramos pedir para cuatro personas. — We would like to order for four people.
- La salsa aparte, por favor. — The sauce separately, please.
- ¿Cuánto hay que esperar? — How long is the wait?
- Podemos llegar más tarde. — We can come later.
- Nuestro plan cambió. — Our plan has changed.
- Confirme el pedido, por favor. — Please confirm the order.

### Teaching notes

- Mention the number of people separately from individual food preferences.
- A preference and a request for a separate sauce are distinct details. Do not infer ingredients that have not been confirmed.
- When changing an order, state which details stay the same and which change.

### Reading source

El sábado quisiéramos pedir cena para cuatro personas. Dos no comen carne y necesitan platillos sin carne. Las otras dos quieren pollo. Sirvan la salsa aparte para todos, por favor. Podemos llegar a las siete. Si hay que esperar más, avísennos por mensaje. Quisiéramos recibir la confirmación del pedido antes de llegar.

English guide:
The Saturday meal is for four: two meat-free meals and two chicken meals. Sauce should be separate for everyone. Arrival is seven; notify the group by message about a longer wait and confirm before arrival.

### Listening source

Buenos días, cambió nuestro plan para la cena. Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media. Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte. ¿Podría confirmar el nuevo número de personas y la hora?

English guide:
The order changes to five people at seven thirty, with three meat-free meals. Two chicken meals and the separate sauce request remain. The caller requests confirmation of the new number and time.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.listen.3039040582a8.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. How many meals without meat are requested?
   - Two **[key]**
   - Three
   - Four
2. How should the sauce be served?
   - Only with chicken
   - Separately for everyone **[key]**
   - Mixed into every dish
3. What should happen before arrival?
   - Cancel every order
   - Change the day
   - Confirm the order **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. How many people will now attend?
   - Four
   - Five **[key]**
   - Six
2. What remains unchanged?
   - Two chicken meals **[key]**
   - The arrival time
   - The number of meat-free meals
3. What is the new arrival time?
   - Seven
   - Eight
   - Seven thirty **[key]**

### Spoken production

Explain a group meal order and a change to it.

- Give the group size.
- Distinguish preferences clearly.
- State a change and ask for confirmation.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Cuántas personas vienen en su grupo?
   - Meaning: How many people are in your group?
   - Response goal: Give the number of people.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.turn-0.79fd7c9b85b0.mp3)
2. ¿Tienen alguna preferencia para los platillos?
   - Meaning: Do you have meal preferences?
   - Response goal: Explain a preference and who it concerns.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.turn-1.b58624118c83.mp3)
3. Hay que esperar media hora. ¿Les parece bien?
   - Meaning: There is a half-hour wait. Does that work?
   - Response goal: Accept the wait or propose an alternative and ask for confirmation.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-meals.turn-2.b23daf152bf6.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Explain the revised order to a friend, preserving the two chicken meals while updating the group size, meat-free meals and time.

### Writing assignments

#### Update a group order

Reader: A restaurant

Write a practical change to an invented group meal order.

Suggested length: 25–90 words.

- Identify the changed group size or time.
- Explain which meal requests change and which remain.
- Request confirmation.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro. Solo podemos estar a las siete y media. Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte. ¿Podría confirmar el nuevo número de personas y la hora?

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Identify the changed group size and time
  - Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro. Solo podemos estar a las siete y media.
  - The changed number and time are explicit; the old number is not silently carried forward.
- Separate changed and unchanged requests
  - Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.
  - This keeps the new meat-free count separate from the unchanged chicken dishes and sauce request.
- Request confirmation
  - ¿Podría confirmar el nuevo número de personas y la hora?
  - The restaurant is asked to confirm the change, rather than being treated as if it had already agreed.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A message to a restaurant about a group order

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### five-seven-chicken

Nuestro plan cambió. Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro. Podemos llegar a las siete. Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte. Confirme el pedido, por favor.

- How many people will now arrive?
  - Model detail: Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro.
  - Four
  - Five **[key]**
  - Three
  - Explanation: The new group size is five; four is the earlier number being replaced.
- Which requests are explicitly kept?
  - Model detail: Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.
  - Two chicken dishes and sauce separately **[key]**
  - Chicken for everyone with the sauce mixed in
  - Only the original group size
  - Explanation: Se mantienen and seguimos preserve those requests while other details change.
- What does the writer request from the restaurant?
  - Model detail: Confirme el pedido, por favor.
  - Cancel the chicken dishes
  - Serve all sauce together
  - Confirm the updated order **[key]**
  - Explanation: The message requests confirmation; it does not report that the restaurant has already agreed.

#### four-seven-thirty

El sábado quisiéramos pedir cena para cuatro personas. Dos no comen carne. Las otras dos quieren pollo. Solo podemos estar a las siete y media. Si hay que esperar más, avísennos por mensaje. Quisiéramos recibir la confirmación del pedido antes de llegar.

- Which meal needs are described?
  - Model detail: Dos no comen carne. Las otras dos quieren pollo.
  - All four people want chicken
  - Three meat-free dishes and two chicken dishes
  - Two people avoid meat and two want chicken **[key]**
  - Explanation: The two groups together make four; this is not the five-person order from another message.
- When can this group arrive?
  - Model detail: Solo podemos estar a las siete y media.
  - Seven-thirty **[key]**
  - Seven
  - Eight-thirty
  - Explanation: Siete y media means seven-thirty.
- What should happen if the wait is longer?
  - Model detail: Si hay que esperar más, avísennos por mensaje.
  - The restaurant must cancel the whole order
  - The restaurant should notify the group by message **[key]**
  - The group has already agreed to wait half an hour
  - Explanation: Si states a condition. It does not establish that a longer wait has already been confirmed.

### Controlled listening transfer

A group meal update or a message about a wait

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### five-eight-update

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2-meals.five-eight-update.2519cccf43e8.mp3)

Buenos días. Nuestro plan cambió. Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro. Solo podemos estar a las ocho. Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte. Confirme el pedido, por favor.

- Which group size and arrival time does this update give?
  - Model detail: Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro. Solo podemos estar a las ocho.
  - Four people at eight
  - Five people at seven-thirty
  - Five people at eight **[key]**
  - Explanation: The new number is five, and this message says eight rather than seven-thirty.
- Which requests are retained?
  - Model detail: Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.
  - Two chicken dishes and the sauce separately **[key]**
  - Chicken for all five people
  - The original group size and arrival time
  - Explanation: The chicken dishes and separate sauce remain while other details change.
- What does the speaker want the restaurant to do?
  - Model detail: Confirme el pedido, por favor.
  - Assume no reply is needed
  - Confirm the order **[key]**
  - Cancel the whole meal
  - Explanation: This asks the restaurant to confirm; it is not a report of a confirmed booking.

#### four-half-hour-wait

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/es.A2-meals.four-half-hour-wait.a7cf9f5d69a9.mp3)

Quisiéramos pedir para cuatro personas. Dos no comen carne. Las otras dos quieren pollo. Hay que esperar media hora. ¿Les parece bien? Podemos llegar más tarde.

- Which meal needs are described?
  - Model detail: Dos no comen carne. Las otras dos quieren pollo.
  - Two people avoid meat and two want chicken **[key]**
  - All four people want chicken
  - Three people avoid meat and two want chicken
  - Explanation: The two groups add up to four people, not five.
- What does the message say about waiting?
  - Model detail: Hay que esperar media hora.
  - There will definitely be no wait
  - A half-hour wait is expected **[key]**
  - The meal has been cancelled
  - Explanation: Unlike a conditional warning about a possible delay, this says a half-hour wait will be needed.
- Has everyone already accepted the wait?
  - Model detail: ¿Les parece bien? Podemos llegar más tarde.
  - Yes, acceptance is explicitly confirmed
  - No, everyone has refused
  - No acceptance is stated; the speaker asks whether it works **[key]**
  - Explanation: The speaker asks whether the arrangement works and offers arriving later.

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A2-meals:group

Question purpose: How many people are in your group?

Prepared question: ¿Cuántas personas vienen en su grupo?

Partner message: Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.

Detail check: Which changed details does the caller give?

Key: Five people at seven-thirty

Optional response frame: Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.

Response goals:
- Restate the changed number and time and ask for confirmation, rather than carrying over the original order.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2-meals:preferences

Question purpose: Do you have meal preferences?

Prepared question: ¿Tienen alguna preferencia para los platillos?

Partner message: Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.

Detail check: Which request is retained alongside the new meat-free count?

Key: Two chicken dishes and sauce separately

Optional response frame: Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.

Response goals:
- Summarise three meat-free dishes, two chicken dishes and separate sauce. Do not invent an allergy or additional requirement.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Check the group size or food preferences before responding to the new order.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: ¿Cuántas personas vienen en su grupo?
  - Meaning: How many people are in your group?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-meals.question-group.79fd7c9b85b0.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-meals.group.78d80444cd14.mp3)

- Checking question: ¿Tienen alguna preferencia para los platillos?
  - Meaning: Do you have meal preferences?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-meals.question-preferences.b58624118c83.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/es.A2-meals.preferences.482e568d886a.mp3)

B1–C2 question frames are optional and their use is saved. Intention selection drives the partner; speech is never interpreted automatically.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: group

Which changed details does the caller give?

Key: Five people at seven-thirty

Own-response goal: Restate the changed number and time and ask for confirmation, rather than carrying over the original order.

Prepared wording: Vamos a llegar cinco personas, no cuatro, y solo podemos estar a las siete y media.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: preferences

Which request is retained alongside the new meat-free count?

Key: Two chicken dishes and sauce separately

Own-response goal: Summarise three meat-free dishes, two chicken dishes and separate sauce. Do not invent an allergy or additional requirement.

Prepared wording: Tres personas necesitan platillos sin carne. Los dos platos de pollo se mantienen. Seguimos queriendo la salsa aparte.

<a id="a2-capstone"></a>
## A2 · Coordinate two arrangements

Unit reference: A2-capstone

### Learning goal

Write two connected updates: explain the appointment arrangement and the work arrangement. Identify what must be confirmed before the plans can work.

Builds on: A2-meals

### Pattern explanation

Keep each arrangement distinct. Use a clear next-step sentence instead of implying that every proposal has already been confirmed.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- cita - appointment [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-0.ced5b5cee531.mp3)
- documentos - documents [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-1.12f85c87e40d.mp3)
- martes - Tuesday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-2.f0c5a6256e4c.mp3)
- viernes - Friday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2.word-5.045fae248e6e.mp3)
- trabajo - work [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-0.9758ba92f3a2.mp3)
- mensajes - messages [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-1.8338db04358d.mp3)
- reunión - meeting [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-2.53e56db23654.mp3)
- cliente - client [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-3.6fc54ed32c31.mp3)

### Additional source language

- ayuda — help
  - Gracias por su ayuda.
- cambiarla — change it, referring to the appointment
  - Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres.
- confirmen — confirm, polite plural instruction
  - Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
- día — day
  - ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?
- escríbanos — write to us, polite usted instruction
  - Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos.
- espacio — available slot / space in the schedule
  - El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde.
- le — to / for you, polite usted in le viene bien
  - Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos.
- llegue — arrive, polite usted instruction
  - Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.
- mensaje — message
  - Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
- minutos — minutes
  - Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.
- nuevo — new, masculine singular
  - Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.
- para — for / in order to
  - Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez.
- poco — a little, in un poco antes
  - Voy a llevar los documentos y llegar un poco antes.
- revisar — review / check
  - Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez.
- su — your, polite usted; or their, according to context
  - Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez.
- tenemos — we have
  - El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde.
- traiga — bring, polite usted instruction
  - Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos.
- avísame — let me know, informal tú instruction
  - Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
- clientes — clients
  - Después llamo a los clientes.
- conectarme — connect / join online, referring to myself
  - Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde.
- equipo — team
  - Primero reviso los mensajes y a las diez me reúno con el equipo.
- espero — I am waiting for, in espero a un técnico
  - Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
- internet — internet
  - Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde.
- juntos — together
  - Por la tarde llamamos juntos a un cliente nuevo.
- lista — list
  - Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor.
- llamamos — we call / will call, in this plan
  - Por la tarde llamamos juntos a un cliente nuevo.
- normalmente — usually / normally
  - Normalmente empiezo a trabajar a las nueve en la oficina.
- prepara — prepare, informal tú instruction
  - Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor.
- reúno — part of me reúno: I meet / get together
  - Primero reviso los mensajes y a las diez me reúno con el equipo.
- ser — be, in va a ser: is going to be
  - Hola, mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.
- terminas — you finish, informal tú
  - Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
- técnico — technician / repair worker
  - Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico.
- Son dos planes distintos. Hay que confirmar la hora y acordar qué haremos después. — These are two different plans. We need to check the time and agree what we will do next.
  - Son dos planes distintos. Hay que confirmar la hora y acordar qué haremos después.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Quisiera hacer una cita. — I would like to make an appointment.
- ¿Tiene algún horario disponible el martes? — Do you have an available time on Tuesday?
- Puedo ir por la tarde. — I can come in the afternoon.
- Lo siento, ese horario no me viene bien. — Unfortunately, that time does not work for me.
- Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. — I start work at nine.
- Primero reviso los mensajes. — First I check messages.
- Después de la reunión voy a llamar al cliente. — After the meeting I will call the client.
- Son dos planes distintos. Hay que confirmar la hora y acordar qué haremos después. — These are two different plans. We need to check the time and agree what we will do next.

### Teaching notes

- Revisit the language and source guides from both preceding cases.
- Keep each arrangement distinct. Use a clear next-step sentence instead of implying that every proposal has already been confirmed.
- Plan for the stated reader. Use the worked model to study purpose, then supply your own response rather than copying it.

### Reading source

Buenos días. Su cita para revisar los documentos es el martes a las diez. Por favor, llegue diez minutos antes y traiga los documentos. Si ese horario no le viene bien, escríbanos. El viernes tenemos un espacio a las tres de la tarde. Debe confirmar el nuevo horario antes del jueves.

Normalmente empiezo a trabajar a las nueve en la oficina. Primero reviso los mensajes y a las diez me reúno con el equipo. Después llamo a los clientes. Como a las doce y media. Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet y llamar a los clientes por la tarde.

English guide:
First written source: The document appointment is Tuesday at ten. Arrive ten minutes early and bring documents. Friday at three in the afternoon is available as a replacement, which must be confirmed before Thursday.

Second written source: Normally the writer starts at nine in the office, checks messages, meets the team at ten and then calls clients. Lunch is at twelve thirty. Today they work at home waiting for a technician, attend online and move client calls to the afternoon.

A useful synthesis pattern

Son dos planes distintos. Hay que confirmar la hora y acordar qué haremos después.

These are two different plans. We need to check the time and agree what we will do next.

### Listening source

Buenos días, llamo por mi cita. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos y llegar un poco antes. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Gracias por su ayuda.

Hola, mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la tarde llamamos juntos a un cliente nuevo. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once. Podemos repartir el trabajo.

English guide:
First spoken source: The caller cannot attend Tuesday morning because of work. They ask for Friday at three, will bring documents and arrive early, and request confirmation by message.

Second spoken source: Tomorrow’s meeting moves from ten to eleven. The listener should prepare a client list; the speaker will check documents. They will call a new client together in the afternoon. If the list will not be ready, the listener should say so before eleven.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-capstone.listen.7511d252be91.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. First written source: What is the original appointment?
   - Tuesday at ten **[key]**
   - Friday at ten
   - Thursday at three
2. Second written source: Why is the writer at home today?
   - Waiting for a technician **[key]**
   - The office has closed permanently
   - They are on holiday
3. Second written source: When is the normal lunch break?
   - At eleven
   - At one thirty
   - At twelve thirty **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. First spoken source: Why does the caller change the booking?
   - They are travelling
   - They have to work **[key]**
   - They lost the address
2. Second spoken source: What is the new meeting time?
   - Ten
   - Eleven **[key]**
   - Twelve
3. Second spoken source: What should happen if the list is not ready?
   - Cancel every call
   - Say nothing until afternoon
   - Notify the speaker before eleven **[key]**

### Spoken production

Write two connected updates: explain the appointment arrangement and the work arrangement. Identify what must be confirmed before the plans can work.

- Explain the appointment and any change.
- Explain the work schedule and responsibilities.
- Make the confirmation or next action clear.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?
   - Meaning: Which day would you like an appointment?
   - Response goal: Request a day and a time of day.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.turn-0.04022d0f7991.mp3)
2. ¿Cómo es normalmente su mañana de trabajo?
   - Meaning: What is your normal working morning like?
   - Response goal: Describe two activities in order.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.turn-0.13d3e1800158.mp3)
3. ¿Cómo podemos repartir las tareas?
   - Meaning: How can we divide the tasks?
   - Response goal: Offer to do one task and ask the partner to do another.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.turn-2.da716c936852.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Explain the two cases to the specified reader. Preserve the differences between the written and spoken versions, and between the two situations. Do not invent a shared person, date, budget or decision.

### Writing assignments

#### Coordinate two arrangements · written response

Reader: A colleague helping to organise your day

Write two connected updates: explain the appointment arrangement and the work arrangement. Identify what must be confirmed before the plans can work.

Suggested length: 55–150 words.

- Explain the appointment and any change.
- Explain the work schedule and responsibilities.
- Make the confirmation or next action clear.


##### Project-specific preparation

Study how the two cases remain separate and how the final sentence serves the reader. Plan, draft and revise your own response. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR threshold.

Worked model:
Mi cita es el martes a las diez. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.

Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

Son dos planes distintos. Hay que confirmar la hora y acordar qué haremos después.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Explain the appointment and any change.
  - Mi cita es el martes a las diez. El martes por la mañana tengo que trabajar y no puedo ir. Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Voy a llevar los documentos. Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje.
  - The first case keeps its own details and qualifications. These model details come from the prepared unit.
- Explain the work schedule and responsibilities.
  - Hoy trabajo desde casa porque espero a un técnico. Empiezo a trabajar a las nueve. Voy a conectarme a la reunión por internet. Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.
  - The second case is presented separately, so its people, conditions and decisions do not silently replace those of the first.
- Make the confirmation or next action clear.
  - Son dos planes distintos. Hay que confirmar la hora y acordar qué haremos después.
  - Keep each arrangement distinct. Use a clear next-step sentence instead of implying that every proposal has already been confirmed.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A2-services:day

Question purpose: Which day would you like an appointment?

Prepared question: ¿Para qué día quisiera la cita?

Partner message: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Por favor, confirme el horario.

Detail check: Which appointment is requested?

Key: Friday at three

Optional response frame: Quisiera cambiarla al viernes a las tres. Por favor, confirme el horario.

Response goals:
- Restate the requested day and time, and distinguish the request from a confirmed appointment.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2-services:confirmation

Question purpose: How would you like confirmation?

Prepared question: ¿Cómo desea recibir la confirmación?

Partner message: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.

Detail check: How does the caller want confirmation?

Key: By a message

Optional response frame: Por favor, confirmen el nuevo horario por mensaje. Voy a llevar los documentos.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge the requested confirmation by message and the documents, without claiming confirmation has already arrived.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2-work:meeting

Question purpose: Tomorrow the meeting starts an hour later. Does that work?

Prepared question: Mañana la reunión empieza una hora más tarde. ¿Le viene bien?

Partner message: Mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.

Detail check: What time is now proposed?

Key: Eleven

Optional response frame: Mañana nuestra reunión va a ser a las once, no a las diez.

Response goals:
- Restate eleven rather than ten and say whether the new time works for you.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2-work:tasks

Question purpose: How can we divide the tasks?

Prepared question: ¿Cómo podemos repartir las tareas?

Partner message: Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

Detail check: What is the listener asked to do?

Key: Prepare the list and report a problem by the stated deadline

Optional response frame: Yo voy a revisar los documentos. Por la mañana prepara la lista de clientes, por favor. Si no terminas la lista, avísame antes de las once.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge your list task, keep the speaker’s document task separate, and mention when you would ask for help.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

<a id="b1"></a>
## B1 · Handle a travel complication

Unit reference: B1

### Learning goal

Give a connected account and explain reasons for a decision.

Builds on: A2-capstone

### Pattern explanation

Al principio and al final frame the account. Sin embargo introduces a contrast. En mi opinión marks a view; por ejemplo introduces supporting illustration.

### Support plan

Use a few keywords to plan. Give your own connected response before comparing it with the source; return to the guide to repair a specific gap.

### Skill targets

- reading: Follow a connected account, its reasons and its practical outcome. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the main points and relevant details of clear speech on a familiar matter. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give a connected account and explain reasons for a decision. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Handle a familiar complication and clarify what to do next. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Summarise practical information and keep confirmed facts separate from proposals. (selected scale: Processing text in speech or sign)
- writing: Write connected text explaining events, reasons and a practical response. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- boletos - tickets [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.B1.word-0.e44953fdbefe.mp3)
- problema - problem [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.B1.word-1.3b9417ab9bd8.mp3)
- ayuda - help [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-work.word-7.e66807f372b5.mp3)
- al final - in the end [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.B1.word-3.ab528b36d649.mp3)
- por ejemplo - for example [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.B1.word-4.82a6cd764116.mp3)
- horario - timetable [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.A2-services.word-6.e754c9ba7590.mp3)
- ruta - route [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.B1.word-6.da4b69be7170.mp3)
- calma - calmness [Hear](../../../audio/skills/es.B1.word-7.b28b665380c0.mp3)

### Additional source language

- El fin de semana pasado viajé con una amiga a otra ciudad. — Last weekend I travelled with a friend to another city.
  - El fin de semana pasado viajé con una amiga a otra ciudad.
- Al principio todo salió según lo previsto: llegamos a la terminal a tiempo y compramos los boletos. — At first everything went as planned: we reached the bus terminal on time and bought the tickets.
  - Al principio todo salió según lo previsto: llegamos a la terminal a tiempo y compramos los boletos.
- El autobús se había cancelado y el siguiente salía varias horas después. — The bus had been cancelled, and the next one was leaving several hours later.
  - El autobús se había cancelado y el siguiente salía varias horas después.
- Una empleada nos explicó que había otra ruta. — An employee explained to us that there was another route.
  - Una empleada nos explicó que había otra ruta.
- Al final encontramos una solución y continuamos el viaje, aunque llegamos más tarde de lo esperado. — In the end we found a solution and continued the journey, although we arrived later than expected.
  - Al final encontramos una solución y continuamos el viaje, aunque llegamos más tarde de lo esperado.
- Aun así, pudimos pasear por el centro y disfrutar de la tarde. — Even so, we were able to walk around the centre and enjoy the afternoon.
  - Aun así, pudimos pasear por el centro y disfrutar de la tarde.
- Esta experiencia me enseñó que no sirve de mucho buscar culpables cuando cambian los planes. — This experience taught me that looking for someone to blame is not very useful when plans change.
  - Esta experiencia me enseñó que no sirve de mucho buscar culpables cuando cambian los planes.
- En mi opinión, conviene prepararse con tiempo y mantener la calma para encontrar una salida. — In my opinion, it is advisable to prepare ahead and stay calm to find a way out.
  - En mi opinión, conviene prepararse con tiempo y mantener la calma para encontrar una salida.
- La próxima vez consultaré el horario antes de salir y tendré en cuenta otra forma de llegar. — Next time I will check the timetable before leaving and consider another way to get there.
  - La próxima vez consultaré el horario antes de salir y tendré en cuenta otra forma de llegar.
- Ayer viajé a un congreso en otra ciudad. — Yesterday I travelled to a conference in another city.
  - Ayer viajé a un congreso en otra ciudad.
- Había comprado el boleto con anticipación, pero en la terminal me dijeron que mi autobús llevaba dos horas de retraso. — I had bought the ticket in advance, but at the bus terminal they told me that my bus was two hours late.
  - Había comprado el boleto con anticipación, pero en la terminal me dijeron que mi autobús llevaba dos horas de retraso.
- Al principio me preocupé mucho porque tenía que hablar antes del mediodía. — At first I became very worried because I had to speak before noon.
  - Al principio me preocupé mucho porque tenía que hablar antes del mediodía.
- Le pedí consejo a una empleada. — I asked an employee for advice.
  - Le pedí consejo a una empleada.
- Me propuso tomar otro autobús y cambiar en un pueblo pequeño. — She suggested taking another bus and changing in a small town.
  - Me propuso tomar otro autobús y cambiar en un pueblo pequeño.
- El viaje costó un poco más, pero llegué a tiempo. — The journey cost a little more, but I arrived on time.
